                    mu. irākavaiyaṅkār avarkaḷiṉ
                      "cēraṉ - ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ"

   source:
       cēraṉ - ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ --- mu. irākavaiyaṅkār -- 1915
       1915
       urimaiyurai.
       cintaivaru pukaḻccēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ
             caritan tikaḻu minnūl
       vintaimiku tarumaturai viyaṉakariṟ
             ṟamiḻccaṅkam viḻaintu kūṭṭic
       centamiḻiṉ vaḷamperukkic ciṟiyēṉum
             perumaipeṟac ceyta vaṇmaic
       cuntaraṉām tamiḻppāṇṭit turaikkurimai
             yākinalan tulaṅka maṉṉō.
                   ākkiyōṉ.
       ----------------

                                  PREFACE.
       It does not require any very detailed statement of the reasons why
       a very correct knowledge and appreciation of the literature of a
       country are needed. For, the literature of a country is a mirror
       which reflects the civilisation and institutions of a country very
       vividly in all its stages of development. That a knowledge of the
       country's past and of the progress or transition is essential by
       way of stimulus to future progress, is conceded by all. The Tamil
       land owes a deep debt of gratitude to Mahamahopadhyaya V.
       Swaminatha Ayer; for re-introducing the great classical epics and
       pieces of heroic poetry, that possess in a pre-eminent degree the
       merits and excellences due to literary masterpieces. Of them
       Pathiruppatu wholly, and Silappathikaram partly, deal with the
       Chera dynasty of the immortal lines of Tamil Kings. The history of
       that dynasty is still shrouded in considerable obscurity. Any works
       that bear upon them, and any exposition of works that would serve
       to enlighten the public as regards their history and and the
       achievements of their Kings in peace and war, any narrative that
       would popularise the stories of those Kings, would meet with a sure
       welcome from the eager Tamil reading public.
       It gives me considerable pleasure to write a short prefatory note
       to the work of my friend who in undertaking to write in an easy
       elegant and classic prose, the life and times of such of the Chera
       Kings as can be gleaned from the said works and from the other
       historical sources has rendered a public service. Mr. Pethachi
       Chettiar's love of Tamil, and his readiness to help Tamil writers,
       and institutions, are well-known to the public. And his
       encouragement of the author in bearing the cost of this publication
       is highly praiseworthy.
       This short work is certain to give a correct insight into the
       social and governmental polity of those times, and in particular
       those of Cheran Senguttuvan. His prowess, his knightly virtues, his
       generous appreciation of his rival kings, his sense of
       responsibilities of a king for the welfare, prosperity and progress
       of his subjects, and his solicitude for the same,his piety, his
       councils of ministers, generals and learned men, and his deference
       to their advice, the equipment of his army and his victorious march
       right up to Himalayas and his generous recognition of the heroism
       of his victorious troops by personally conferring on them
       decorations and honours on the banks of the Ganges are described by
       Mr. M.Raghava Ayengar in the present work in easy and interesting
       prose with apt quotations at intervals. The major portion of his
       work is based on some of the chapters of the third book of
       Silappathikaram.The illuminating commentary of Adiyarkunallar not
       being available for the Vanchikkandam of Silappathikaram,the
       students that seek to study that portion of the epic are sure to
       find instruction and guidance in the pages of this book.And it goes
       without saying that Silappthikaram is itself a unique work in the
       literature of this world; for we have no instance of a similar work
       in any other literature by a Royal author who had given up the
       worldly pursuits, and taken holy orders, and yet was called upon to
       compose an epic dealing with many lay and temporal institutions,
       pleasures, and pastimes, the lives and habits and ideals of various
       castes and professions,in commemorating the life of an ideally
       chaste and virtuous woman. Nor can we find a similar instance
       anywhere of a same Royal author dealing impartially with the life
       and times of his brother and King Senguttuvan as also those of his
       rival kings.The moral fervour and the aesthetic* perfection of that
       work are unsurpassed;and the descriptions of natural scenery are
       vivid and his narration of the story is entrancing. A stimulus to
       the study of the work and similar works is greatly added to by Mr.
       M. Raghava Ayengar in writing and publishing this work.
       His dedication of the book to the memory of the founder and first
       President of the Madura Tamil Sangam, while it expresses the
       author's gratitude for him is also appropriate in illustrating the
       principle for which late Mr. Pandithurai Thevar, lived and worked,
       namely, the popularisation and improvement of the Tamil Language
       and Literature. Further Mr. Pandithurai's labours in the
       vindication of the claims of the Tamil Language and Literature
       somewhat bear an analogy to the achievement of the hero of this
       work in vindicating the greatness of the Tamil Kings of those
       times.
       Madura T.C. SREENIVASA AYENGAR,
               Secretary,
       31-8-1915 The Madura Tamil Sangam
       -----------------------------------------------------------

   ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ tantaiyum avaṉ māṟṟān tāyc cakōtararum
   cērariṉ maṟṟoru kiḷaiyiṉar, ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ cakōtarar
   maṉaivimakkaḷ mutaliyōr, cuṟṟattār
   ati-3 ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ pōrcceyalkaḷ
   ati-4 iraṇṭu carita nikaḻccikaḷ
   1. kaṇṇaki varalāṟu
   2. maṇimēkalai varalāṟu
   ati -5 ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ vaṭanāṭṭiyāttirai
   1. vēṭuvar taṅkaḷ kuṟicciyiṟ kaṇṇaki poruṭṭuk kuravaikkūttāṭiyatu
   2. ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ imayattiṉiṉṟu pattiṉikkalleṭuttuvara eṇṇiyatu
   3. vaṭanāṭṭiṟceytapōrum,pattiṉikkuk kalleṭuppittatum
   4 pattiṉippaṭivattaik kaṅkainīrāṭṭit taṉṉāṭu tirumputal
   ati-6 ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ pattiṉikkaṭavuḷaip piratiṣṭittal
   1. naṭukal
   2. ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ pattiṉiyai avaḷuṟṟārōṭum vāḻttiyatu
   3. pattiṉi ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉaiyum piṟaraiyum anukkirakittatu
   ati- 7 ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ camayanilai
   ati- 8 camakālattaracar
   1. cōḻar
   2. pāṇṭiyar
   3. cērar kiḷaiyiṉar
   4. vēṟaracarkaḷ
   ati- 9. ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉaip pāṭiya iruperumpulavarkaḷ
   1. paraṇar
   2. cāttaṉār
   ati- 10 ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ nāṭum- vañcimānakaramum
   ati-11 araciyal
   ati-12 kuṇāticayaṅkaḷ

   ati-14 muṭivurai

                                 mukavurai.
       cēraṉ-ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉaippaṟṟi muṉṉūlkaḷiṟkaṇṭa viṣayaṅkaḷai,
       navīnamuṟaiyil ārāyntu oru carittiramākat tokutteḻutavēṇṭum eṉpatu
       eṉatu neṭunāḷavāākum. iccēraṉai nāṉ eṭuttukkoṇṭataṟku iraṇṭu
       kāraṇaṅkaḷ uṇṭu; mutalāvatu--paṇṭaittamiḻvēntaruḷḷē ivaṉ perumai
       peṟṟavaṉātalōṭu, ēṉait tamiḻaracariṉum ivaṉatu varalāṟu ciṟitu
       atikamākavum kāṇappaṭṭatu. iraṇṭāvatu-- eṉṉārāycciyiṟkaṇṭa
       cilakaruttukkaḷai veḷiyiṭutaṟku ivaṉ carittiramē
       ēṟṟatāyiruntatākum. ivviruvakaiyālum nikaḻnta eṉ ciṟuviruppattai
       ippōtu kaikūṭṭuvitta tiruvaruḷaiccintittu vantikkiṉṟēṉ.
       ivvārāyccikkuc ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉaippaṟṟiya cilappatikāra vañcikkāṇṭam,
       ciṟanta karuviyāyāyiṟṟu.aṭiyārkkunallārurai ippakutikkuk
       kiṭaiyātatu vicaṉikkattakkatāyiṉum, avarkkum muṟpaṭṭa
       arumpatavuraiyoṉṟu veḷivantiruppatu oruvāṟu makiḻattakkatē.
       ivvarumpatavuraiyaip perumpāṉmai taḻuvi, akkāṇṭattiṉ ceyyuṇaṭaiyai
       iyaṉṟaḷavil urainaṭaip-paṭuttalāṉēṉ. centamiḻvaḷañ ceṟintuḷḷa
       iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷatu 'paḻutaṟṟa muttamiḻiṉ pāṭaṟ
       kuraiyiṉ-ṟeḻutattoṭaṅkiṉēṉ" illaiyāyiṉum, avvaṭikaḷatu arumperuṅ
       karuttukkaḷait tamiḻapimāṉikaḷellām aṟintu makiḻavēṇṭum eṉṉum
       pēravāvē immuyaṟciyil eṉṉaittūṇṭiyatu.
       Page 2
       ivvurai naṭaiyai "vaṭanāṭṭiyāttirai" "pattiṉik kaṭavuḷaip
       piratiṣṭittal" eṉṟa iraṇṭatikāraṅkaḷālum aṟiyalām. "vañcimānakaram"
       "ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ kālavārāycci" eṉṟa viṣayaṅkaḷ vivātappaṭṭavaiyātalāl,
       avaṟṟaippaṟṟiya eṉṉapippirāyaṅkaḷaic ciṟitu viritteḻutal
       āvaciyakamāyiṟṟu. ivvatikāraṅkaḷil vivakarikkappaṭṭa eṉ koḷkaikaḷai
       aṟiñar cōtittukkoḷvārkaḷāyiṉ, tamiḻccarittirattiṉ mukkiyamāṉa
       pakutiyoṉṟu muṭivu peṟṟatākum.
       ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉaippaṟṟi ārāyvataṟku iṉṟiyamaiyātakaruvikaḷellām,
       evarkaḷ vāyilāka namakkuk kiṭaittaṉavō, apperiyārai iṅkē
       maṟavātuvantittal nam kaṭamaiyākum. mahāmahōpātyāya-aiyaravarkaḷ
       nam pāṣaikkup purintuḷḷa mahōpakāramaṉṟō, ittakaiya
       ārāyccikaṭkellām kāraṇamākum? ivvārāyccikku iṉṟiyamaiyāta
       akanāṉūṟṟukkuṟippukkaḷai utaviya sētu samstāṉavitvāṉ srī-rā.
       irākavaiyaṅkār avarkaṭku eṉ vantaṉaṅkaḷai samarppikkiṉṟēṉ.
       ivaṟṟuṭaṉ yāṉeḻutiya icciṟunūlai apimāṉittut tammiyaṟkaiyāṉa
       utārakuṇattālum tamiḻapimāṉattālum itaṉ patippukku vēṇṭiya
       utavipurinta srīmān. S.Rm. M. Ct. pettācci ceṭṭiyāravarkaḷ
       peruntakaimai eṉṉāl orupoḻutum maṟakkattakkataṉṟu
       Page 3
       iccarittiranāyakaṉāṉa cērarperumāṉ vīṟṟiruntāṭcipurinta
       vañcimānakariṉ paripālaṉattalaimai pūṇṭuḷḷa ippirapuvukku, avaṉatu
       carittira nūlai ātarikkum urimaiyum iyalpiluṇṭaṉṟō? ivarkaḷ,
       aṟivutiruvāṟṟalkaḷuṭaṉ nīṭuvāḻntu tamiḻ vaḷarcci purintuvarumpaṭi
       tiruvaruḷperukuvatāka.
       iccarittiranāyakaṉāl teyvamāka vaṇaṅkappeṟṟa pattiṉitēvi
       (kaṇṇaki)yiṉ cepputtirumēṉi yoṉṟu "laṇṭaṉ-piriṭṭiṣ-miyūciya"ttil
       iruntatai ṭākṭar.
       ānantakumāracāmiyavarkaḷ piraticāyaiyeṭuttup
       piracurittirukkiṟārkaḷ.* appiratimai, ilaṅkaiyiṉiṉṟu 1830-m varuṣam
       iṅkilāntukkuk koṇṭu pōkappaṭṭatām. appattiṉitēvi paṭivattukku oru
       piratiyeṭuttu innūluṭ cērttirukkiṟēṉ. iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ kūṟiyapaṭi
       ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ kālattē ilaṅkaiyiṟ kayavākuvāl piratiṣṭikkappaṭṭa
       pattiṉiyiṉ cariyāṉa cāyalai itu kāṭṭuvatupōlum.

       *Selected Examples of Indian Art, Plate XXXIII, Pattini Devi.
       Page 4
       cilappatikāra maṇimēkalaippatippukaḷil, aiyaravarkaḷāl
       naṉkeḻutappaṭṭuḷḷa kaṇṇaki maṇimēkalaikaḷiṉ carittirac
       curukkaṅkaḷaip perumpāṉmai taḻuviyē, "ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ kālattu iraṇṭu
       caritanikaḻccikaḷ" eṉṟa atikāram varaiyappaṭṭatu.
       ippatippiṉiṟutiyiṟ cērttirukkum anupanta mutaliyavaikaḷai nūluṭaṉ
       cērttup
       paṭittuk koḷḷutal mukkiyamākum. ivaṟṟuḷ, poruṭkuṟippilē cila
       arumpataṅkaṭku aruttaṅkaḷum kāṭṭap paṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.
       innūṟpatippaic ciṟappittaṟ kuriyavaṟṟai yellām aṉpuṭaṉ ceytutaviya
       maturait tamiḻccaṅkattārkku eṉṟum naṉṟiyaṟitaṟ kaṭappāṭuṭaiyēṉ.

       page 1
       cēraṉ - ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ.
       ~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~
       mutalām atikāram:-
       muṉṉurai.
       =========
       puṇṇiya pūmiyākiya ipparatakaṇṭattuḷḷa purātaṉa rājyaṅkaḷuḷḷē, cēra
       cōḻa pāṇṭiya nāṭukaḷ, takṣiṇattil viḷaṅkiya mukkiya tēcaṅkaḷeṉpatu
       yāvarum aṟintatē. ivvaraciyalkaḷai mutaṉmutal nāṭṭiya muṉṉōr
       iṉṉāreṉṟēṉum, ivai tāpikkappeṟṟa kālam iḵteṉṟēṉum ituvarai evarum
       aḷantu kaṇṭavarallar. vāṉmīkam pōṉṟa purātaṉa vaṭamoḻik
       kāviyaṅkaḷilum, acōka
       cakkaravarttiyatupōṉṟa āti cilālikitaṅkaḷilum immūṉṟu
       tamiḻnāṭṭāraiyum paṟṟik kauravamāṉa pirastāpaṅkaḷē kēṭkappaṭutalāl,
       iṉṉōratu paḻamaiyum perumaiyum aiyamiṉṟi aṟiñarkaḷāṟ
       koḷḷappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. curuṅka uraikkumiṭattu, 'paṭaippukkālantoṭṭu
       mēmpaṭṭuvaruṅ kuṭikaḷ im mūvēntarum' eṉṟu parimēlaḻakiyār eḻutiya
       muṟaiyē* nāmuṅkūṟutal poruntu meṉalām. iv vēntarkaḷāṭcikku uṭpaṭṭa
       nilapparappu muḻutum 'vaṭavēṅkaṭan teṉkumari'kaṭku iṭaippaṭṭatākum.
       "vaṇpukaḻmūvar taṇpoḻilvaraippiṉ" †eṉṟār tolkāppiyaṉārum. in
       nilapparappu, tamiḻnāṭu, tamiḻakam** eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭum.
       -------------
       *tiruvaḷḷuvar. kuṭimai. 5 urai.
       † tolkāppiyam.poruḷati. ceyyu.79
       ** cilap.3.37; patiṟṟup.2-m patikam; puṟam-168.
       Page 2
       iṅṅaṉam paḻamaiyum perumaiyum paṭaitta cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyarkaḷuḷ,
       mutaṟkaṇuḷḷavarē iv vārāyccikku ēṟpuṭaiyavar. ivarkaḷ, cērar
       cēralar eṉat tamiḻiṉum, kēraḷar eṉa vaṭamoḻiyiṉum vaḻaṅkappaṭuvar.
       iv vēntaratu pūrvacarittiramāṉatu, ēṉaic
       cōḻapāṇṭiyaruṭaiyavaipōlavē cevvitiṉ aṟiyappaṭṭilatēṉum, paṇṭai
       yilakkiyaṅkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭumaḷavil, ivaratu
       arumaiyum perumaiyum vēṇṭiyavaḷavu viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. centamiḻ
       vaḷarttavar eṉṟa perumai pāṇṭiyarkkuriyatākap
       paḻaiyavaḻakkuḷḷateṉpatu uṇmaiyē. āyiṉum, taṟkālattu veḷivantuḷḷa
       caṅkanūlkaḷai ārāyvōmāyiṉ, tamiḻvaḷartta perumpukaḻiṟ cērarun takka
       pakuti peṟutaṟkuriyar eṉpatōṭu, aṟivutiruvāṟṟalkaḷil iṉṉōr ēṉait
       tamiḻvēntarkkuk kuṟaintavarallar eṉpatum pulappaṭum. iṅṅaṉam
       nūlkaḷiṟkaṇṭa cērar perumaiyaṉaittaiyum iṅkē vivarippatu em
       karuttaṉṟu. āṉāl, iv vamicattavaruḷḷē, kaṭaiccaṅkanāḷil viḷaṅkiya
       piracitta vēntaṉoruvaṉait tamiḻaṟiñarum carittira nūlōrum
       cilakālamāka naṉku terintirukkiṉṟaṉar. ivaṉ peyar cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ
       eṉpatu. tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ paḻaiya nilaikaḷai ārāyappukunta paṇṭitar
       cilar tamiḻc caritavuṇmaicilavaṟṟaik kaṇṭu veḷiyiṭṭataṟkup
       pērutaviyāka niṉṟatu, ic cēravēntaṉatu varalāṟēyām.
       Page 3
       ataṉāl, vaḻiticai teriyāmalirunta tamiḻccaritat tuṟaiyoṉṟaik
       kāṭṭiniṟkum tīpastampamāka ic ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉaik kūṟal takum.
       ivvaracaṉatu varalāṟukaḷāka muṉṉūlkaḷuṭ kūṟappaṭṭavaṟṟai orucēra
       ārāyvatāṉāl, cēravamicattiṉ paḻaiya ceytikaḷ maṭṭumiṉṟi, tamiḻc
       carittirattiṉ mukkiyamāṉa pakutiyaiyum nammavar aṟintukoḷḷa
       iṭamuṇṭām. āṉāl, ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ carittirattiṟkup paḻaiya nūlkaḷ
       cilavēyaṉṟic cāsaṉa utaviyoṉṟum ituvarai namakkuk kiṭaittilatu.
       itaṉāl ettuṇai ilakkiyap piramāṇaṅkaḷaikkoṇṭu viḷakkiṉum, cāsaṉa
       paricīlaṉai oṉṟēyuṭaiyārkku nammārāycciyil muḻu nampikkaiyum
       uṇṭātal aritēyām. āyiṉum, paṇṭai nūlkaḷ oṟṟumaiyuṭaṉ
       kūṟuñceytikaḷai matittut taḻuvikkoḷḷutal āvaciyakameṉpatai
       nallaṟiñarevarum maṟukkār. paḻaiyavilakkiyaṅkaḷiṟ kaṇṭa
       carittiravuṇmaikaḷ kālāntarattiṟ kiṭaikkum cāsaṉavutaviyāl
       valipeṟṟu varutalai nām kaṇṭuvarutalāl, caṅkac ceyyuḷkaḷaiyē
       mukkiya ātāramākakkoṇṭu nikaḻttappeṟum ivvārāycciyum
       payaṉṟarattakkateṉṟē nampukiṉṟōm.
       iṉi, ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉatu varalāṟukaḷai aṟivataṟkuc cātaṉamākak
       kiṭaittuḷḷa ilakkiyakkaruvikaḷai nōkkumiṭattu, avai peritum
       matippukkuriyaṉa eṉpatil aiyamillai. eṉṉeṉiṉ, iv vēntar perumāṉuṭaṉ
       piṟantavarāṉa iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ tāmiyaṟṟiya cilappatikārattil oru kāṇṭa
       muḻutum, tam tamaiyaṉatu vāḻkkaiyiṉ oru pakutiyait tām
       nērilaṟintavāṟu viḷakkiyirukkiṉṟār. ivvāṟu veṟṟivēnta ṉoruvaṉ
       ceytikaḷ avaṉ cakōtararālē viritturaikkappaṭumāyiṉ nām avaṟṟai
       nampat taṭaiyumuṇṭō?
       Page 4
       aṉṟiyum, aṅṅaṉam pāṭiyavar tamatu perumpataviyaiyum tuṟantuniṉṟa
       rājaruṣiyāyiruppatu, avaratu vākkiṉ ṟūymaiyaiyē vaṟpuṟuttavallatu;
       "muṭikeḻu vēntar mūvarkku muriyatu - aṭikaḷ nīrē yaruḷuka" eṉṟu,
       avaratu naṭunilaiyaip pukaḻntaṉar avar kālattup pulavaroruvarum.
       itaṟkēṟpa, ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ varalāṟu paṟṟiya cilappatikāra
       vañcikkāṇṭattaip paṭittu varuvōrevarkkum kaṟpaṉaikaḷ kalavātatu
       appakuti-yeṉpatum viḷaṅkat taṭaiyillai. iṉi, ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉālum
       iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷālum naṉkumatikkappeṟṟavarum pulavar perumāṉumākiya
       cāttaṉārāl iyaṟṟappeṟṟa maṇimēkalai yeṉṉum nūliṉum, iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ
       kūṟiyavaṟṟōṭotta ceytikaḷ pala kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟuṭaṉ, paḻaiya
       pulavar palarāl cēravaracar perumaikaḷ pukaḻntu kūṟappaṭṭa
       patiṟṟuppattu eṉṉun tokainūluḷ, paraṇar pāṭiya
       aintāmpattumuḻumaiyum iccēraṉaippaṟṟi amaintuḷḷatu. ippattiṉai,
       appulavarperumāṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ muṉṉarppāṭi, avaṉāl mikutiyum
       cammāṉikkappeṟṟavar eṉpatu, ataṉiṟuti vākkiyaṅkaḷāl teḷivākiṉṟatu.
       ākavē, iv vārāyccikkuc cilappatikāram, maṇimēkalai, patiṟṟuppattu
       eṉpavai ciṟanta cātaṉaṅkaḷākum. ivaiyaṉṟic caṅkac ceyyuḷkaḷ cilavum
       vēṟu puṟakkaruvikaḷum uriyavaḷavil upakārappaṭuvaṉa. iṅṅaṉaṅ
       kiṭaittuḷḷa cātaṉaṅkaḷaikkoṇṭu ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ varalāṟṟaiyum, avaṉ
       muṉṉōraip paṟṟiya ceytikaḷaiyum, avaṉ kālattu aracar pulavar
       caritaṅkaḷaiyum, maṟṟum pala nikaḻcci nilaimaikaḷaiyum iṉi
       virittukkūṟa muyalvōm.

       itu, muṟkālattē cuḷḷiyeṉṉum pēriyāṟu kaṭaluṭaṉ kalakkumiṭattu

       cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ
       tantaiyum avaṉ māṟṟāntāyc cakōtararum.
       ------------------------------------------------
       mēṟkūṟiya cērarkaṭkum ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉukku muḷḷa toṭarpai iṉi ārāyvōm.
       ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉaip paṟṟiya aintām pattup* patikattuḷ, avaṉ,
       "vaṭavaruṭkum vāṉṟōy nallicaik - kuṭavar kōmāṉ neṭuñ cēralātaṉukku"
       makaṉ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṉṟāṉ. itaṉāl iraṇṭāmpattiṟ pukaḻappaṭum
       imayavarampaṉ – neṭuñcēralātaṉē nam cēraṉ tantai eṉpatu viḷaṅkum.
       "āriyar tuvaṉṟiya pēricai yimayan - teṉṉaṅ kumariyo ṭāyiṭai, maṉmīk
       kūṟunar maṟantapak kaṭantē" † eṉa ivaṉatu vaṭaticai veṟṟiyai
       avviraṇṭāmpattē kūṟutal kāṇka. ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ uṭaṉpiṟantavarāṉa
       iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ, imayam varai veṟṟippukaḻ parappiya tam tantai
       ceyalai, "kumariyoṭu vaṭavimayat – toru moḻivait- tulakāṇṭa
       cēralātaṟku" eṉak kūṟutalum iṅku aṟiyat takkatu.

       * iṅṅaṉaṅ kuṟikkumiṭamellām, patiṟṟup pattiṉaiyē koḷka.

       nalliṉiyiṭam piṟantavaṉ. ivaṉatu ariya ceyalkaḷāvaṉa:--imayamvarai
       paṭaiyeṭuttuc ceṉṟu ammalaimēl taṉ ilāñcaṉaiyākiya villaip
       poṟittatu; tamiḻaka muḻumaiyun taṉ ceṅkōliṉ kīḻ vaittāṇṭatu;
       taṉṉuṭaṉ poruta āriyavaracarai veṉṟuavarai vaṇaṅkac ceytatu; yavaṉa

       *ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉai avaṉ cakōtarar - "mānīr vēlik kaṭampaṟuttimayattu,

       collappaṭṭuḷḷatu. neṭuñcēralātaṉukkuc ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉuṭaṉ iḷaṅkō
       oruvaṉum piṟantiruntaṉaṉ. ivviḷaṅkō,pēraṟivum uttamakuṇaṅkaḷum
       vāyntavaṉ. orunāḷ pērattāṇiyil maṉṉarkaḷ puṭaicūḻa, neṭuñcēralātaṉ
       taṉ makkaḷiruvaruṭaṉum vīṟṟiruntapōtu, nikaḻvatu kūṟavalla
       nimittikaṉoruvaṉ am maṇṭapattai aṭaintu,aracaṉaiyum, avaṉ
       makkaḷaiyum aṭimutaṉ muṭivarai nōkki- "vēntar vēntē! iṉi nī
       viṇṇulaku celluṅkālam neruṅkiyatu; nī tāṅkiyuḷḷa ceṅkōlai niṉ
       makkaḷiruvaruḷ iḷaiyōṉē vakittaṟkuriyaṉāvaṉ" eṉṟu palarumaṟiyak
       kūṟiṉāṉ.
       Page 22
       itukēṭṭa mūttavaṉāṉa ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ maṉamuḷaintu niṟka, aḵtaṟinta
       iḷaṅkō, annimittikaṉ muṟaimaikeṭac coṉṉataṟkāka avaṉaivekuṇṭu tan
       tamaiyaṉkoṇṭa maṉakkavalainīṅkumpaṭi avvattāṇikkaṇṇē
       aracāḷurimaiyai muṉintu tuṟavupūṇṭu,vīṭṭulakattai āḷaṟkuriya
       peruvēntarāy viḷaṅkiṉar-eṉpatām. karuvilē tiruvuṭaiyarāyiruntum
       iḷamaiyil tām tuṟavupūṇumpaṭi nērntatait tam aḻakiya vākkāl iv
       viḷaṅkōvē kūṟiyiruttal aṟintu makiḻaṟpālatu. ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉuṭaṉ
       pattiṉikkaṭavuḷai vaḻipaṭṭut tirumpumpōtu, tēvantiyiṉmēl akkaṭavuḷ
       āvēcittut tammainōkkik kūṟiyatāka ivvaṭikaḷ kūṟuvatāvatu:-
       "vēḷvic cālaiyil vēntaṉ peyarntapiṉ
       yāṉuñ ceṉṟēṉ, eṉṉeti reḻuntu
       tēvan tikaimēṟ ṟikaḻntu tōṉṟi
       'vañci mūtūr maṇimaṇ ṭapattiṭai
       nuntai tāṇiḻa liruntōy! niṉṉai
       aracuvīṟ ṟirukkun tiruppoṟi yuṇṭeṉṟu
       uraicey tavaṉmē luruttu nōkkik
       koṅkaviḻ naḻuntārk koṭittērt tāṉaic
       ceṅkuṭ ṭuvaṉṟaṉ cellal nīṅkap
       pakalcel vāyiṟ paṭiyōr tammuṉ
       akaliṭap pāram akala nīkkic
       cintai cellāc cēṇeṭun tūrattu
       antami liṉpat taracāḷ vēntu'eṉa
       eṉṟiṟa muraittaimaiyō riḷaṅkoṭi"(cilappatikāram 30: 170-183.)
       eṉpatu.
       ivvaralāṟṟaiyē aṭiyārkkunallār cilappatikārap patikavuraiyiṟ ciṟitu
       virittukkūṟiṉar.
       Page 23
       ivaṟṟāl, ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ paṭṭameytuvataṟku muṉpu nikaḻnta iḷaṅkōviṉ
       tuṟavuvaralāṟu veḷiyām. iṅṅaṉam tuṟavu pūṇṭa piṉ, iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ eṉa
       vaḻaṅkappeṟṟu, vañcimānakariṉ kīḻpāluḷḷa 'kuṇavāyiṟ kōṭṭam'
       eṉṟaviṭattil vacittu vantaṉar.('kuṇavāyiṟ kōṭṭat taracutuṟan
       tirunta-kuṭakkōc cēraliḷaṅkōvaṭikaṭku' eṉpatu patikam.) ivar, perun
       tuṟaviyāka iruntatu maṭṭumaṉṟi, akkālattirunta uttama kavikaḷuḷ
       oruvarākavum viḷaṅkiṉar. tamiḻaimperuṅ kāviyaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟākac
       ciṟappikkappaṭum cilappatikāram ivvaṭikaḷ iyaṟṟiyateṉpataiyum, ataṉ
       coṟporuḷvaḷaṅkaḷaiyum aṟintu viyavāta aṟiñarumuḷarō? innūlai aṭikaḷ
       pāṭutaṟkuk karuviyāka niṉṟa perumpulavar, maturaikkūlavāṇikaṉ
       cāttaṉār(ivar varalāṟṟai 'iruperum pulavar' eṉṉum innūṟpakutiyuṭ
       kāṇka.) eṉpavar. ippulavarait talaimaiyākakkoṇṭa avaikkaṇṇē aṭikaḷ
       tam nūlai araṅkēṟṟiṉar.("uraicā laṭika ḷaruḷa maturaik-kūlavāṇikaṉ
       cāttaṉ kēṭṭaṉaṉ." (cilap-pati.88-9)) avvac camayanilaikaḷaiyum,
       tamiḻmūvēntar perumaikaḷaiyum naṭunilaipiṟaḻātu kūṟiccelvatilum
       iyaṟkaic ciṟappukkaḷai eṭuttumoḻivatilum, ulakattiṉ tarmaṅkaḷai
       uṇarttuvatilum ivvaṭikaṭkirunta pērāṟṟal mikavum viyakkaṟpālatu.
       ivvaṭikaḷ jainamatattiṟ paṟṟuḷḷavarākavē ivaratu vākkiṉ pōkkāl
       uyttuṇarap paṭumāyiṉum,vaitika camayattavarākak karutaṟkuriya
       cāṉṟukaḷumuḷḷaṉa.
       Page 24
       eṅṅaṉamāyiṉum, matāntaraṅkaḷil apimāṉamum āḻnta ārāycciyumikkavar
       ivvaṭikaḷ eṉpatil aiyamillai.maṟṟum ivar arumai perumaikaḷ innūlil
       uriyaviṭaṅkaḷiṟ kūṟiyuḷḷōm.
       ---------------
       ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ maṉaivimakkaḷ mutaliyōr.
       cēraṉ - ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉuṭaiya kōpperuntēviyāka viḷaṅkiyavaḷ
       iḷaṅkōvēṇmāḷ eṉpāḷ. " iḷaṅkōvēṇmā ḷuṭaṉirun taruḷi"
       (cilappatikāram. 25,5)"vatuvaivēṇmāḷ maṅkala
       maṭantai"(cilappatikāram 28:51) eṉac cilappatikāram kuṟippatu
       kāṇka. itaṉāl, ipperuntēvi vēḷir kulattavaḷ eṉpatu peṟappaṭum.
       ivaḷoruttiyaiyaṉṟi vēṟu maṉaiviyaraic ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ
       maṇantiruntavaṉākat teriyavillai. (iḷaṅkō vēṇmāḷ eṉṟa peyarāl
       ivaḷai iḷaiya maṉaiviyākak karutakkūṭumēṉum, ceṅkuṭṭuvaṟku
       mūttamaṉaiviyorutti iruntatāka iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ uriyaviṭaṅkaḷilum
       uṇarttāmaiyāl, avvāṟu tuṇiyakkūṭavillai. orukāl mūttavaḷiruntu
       iṟantaṉaḷāka, aṭuttumaṇam puriyappeṟṟa ivaḷ iṅṅaṉam vaḻaṅkap
       peṟṟāḷpōlum.) ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉukkuk kuṭṭuvañcēral
       (kuṭṭuvañcēral-(ceṅ)kuṭṭuvaṉukku makaṉākiya cēral eṉapporuḷpaṭum.)
       eṉṉum peyarpūṇṭa makaṉoruvaṉiruntaṉaṉ.
       Page 25
       paraṇareṉṉum pulavar perumāṉ aintām pattāl taṉṉaip pāṭiyatukēṭṭu
       makiḻnta ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ, avvantaṇarkkup perumporuḷōṭu,
       ikkuṭṭuvañcēralaiyum paricāka aḷittāṉeṉṟu ap pattiṉ iṟutivākkiyaṅ
       kūṟukiṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉam 'makaṉaip paricaḷittāṉ’ eṉpatu, paraṇariṭaṅ
       kaṟṟu vallaṉāmpaṭi avaṉaik kurukulavācañ ceyya niyamittataik
       kuṟippatupōlum. ik kuṭṭuvañcēral mēṟkuṟitta kōpperuntēviyiṭam
       piṟantavaṉātal vēṇṭum. immakaṉ paṭṭam peṟṟa
       piṉṉar,muṟkālamuṟaippaṭi vēṟupeyarum ivaṉukku vaḻaṅkiyiruttal
       kūṭum. orukāl, cōḻaṉ perunaṟkiḷḷi, pāṇṭiyaṉ
       ukkirapperuvaḻuti--ivaruṭaṉ naṭpuppūṇṭirunta cēramāṉ māveṇkō
       eṉpavaṉ,(puṟanāṉūṟu--367.) ikkuṭṭuvañcēralō eṉṟu karututaṟkum
       iṭamuṇṭu.
       ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ tāyuṭaṉ piṟanta ammāṉ kāriyāṟṟut tuñciya neṭuṅkiḷḷi
       eṉpavaṉeṉṟum, av vammāṉukku makaṉ cōḻaṉ-irācacūyam vēṭṭa
       perunaṟkiḷḷiyeṉṟum pulappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. itaṟkuṟṟa kāraṇaṅkaḷaic
       "ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ kālattaracar" eṉṟa atikārattuḷ viḷakkiyuḷḷōm.ivvāṟu
       paṇṭainūlkaḷāṟ ṟerintavaḷaviṟ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ cuṟṟattāraip piṉṉar
       varumāṟu tokuttuk kāṭṭalām. ivaraippaṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷai āṅkāṅku
       aṟintukoḷka.
       -------------
       page 26
       [ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ. ... ... cuṟṟattār.]
       1. tantaiyaippeṟṟa pāṭṭaṉ -- utiyañcēral.
       2. tantaiyaippeṟṟa pāṭṭi -- vēṇmāḷ nalliṉi.
       3. tantai -- imayavarampaṉ-neṭuñcēralātaṉ.
       4. tāy -- naṟcōṇai.
       5. iḷaiya cakōtarar -- iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ.
       6. maṉaivi -- iḷaṅkōvēṇmāḷ.
       7. makaṉ -- kuṭṭuvañcēral.
       8. ciṟiyatantai -- palyāṉaic celkeḻu kuṭṭuvaṉ.
       9. tāyppāṭṭaṉ -- cōḻaṉ maṇakkiḷḷi.
       10. ammāṉ -- kāriyāṟṟut tuñciya neṭuṅkiḷḷi.
       11. ammāṉ cēy -- irācacūyam vēṭṭa perunaṟkiḷḷi.
       12. māṟṟāntāy -- patumaṉṟēvi.
       13. māṟṟāntāy vayiṟṟuc cakōtarar -- nārmuṭiccēral.
       āṭukōṭpāṭṭuc cēralātaṉ.
       14. māṟṟāntāyp pāṭṭaṉ -- vēḷāvikkōmāṉ patumaṉ.
       15. tāyattār -- irumpoṟai marapiṉar.
       -----------------------------------------------------------
       page 27
       3-m atikāram:-
       ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ pōrcceyalkaḷ
       neṭuñcēralātaṉ viṇṇulakañ ceṉṟapōtu ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉukku uttēcam
       20-vayatu koḷḷalām. āyiṉ,55-āṇṭu ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ vīṟṟiruntatākap
       patiṟṟuppattuk kūṟutalāl, kuṟaintatu 35-varuṣakālam ivaṉ āṭci
       ceytavaṉātal vēṇṭum. innīṭitta āṭciyil nikaḻnta ivaṉ ceytikaḷ
       muḻutun teriyak kūṭavillai. āyiṉum,cilappatikāramum patiṟṟuppattum,
       ivaṉāl nikaḻttappaṭṭa aruñceyalkaḷ cilavaṟṟaik
       kuṟittirukkiṉṟaṉa.avaṟṟiṟkaṇṭa ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ vīracceyalkaḷilē
       ciṟappuṭaiyavai aṭiyil varuvaṉavām.
       1. taṉ tāyiṉporuṭṭuc camaitta paṭimattaik kaṅkainīrāṭṭac ceṉṟapōtu,
       āṅketirtta āriyavaracaruṭaṉ naṭattiya pōr.
       2. koṅkar ceṅkaḷattu naṭattiya pōr
       3. kaṭalvaḻiyē ceṉṟu naṭattiya pōr
       4. paḻaiyaṉ māṟaṉuṭaṉ naṭattiya pōr
       5. oṉpatu cōḻaruṭaṉ naṭattiya pōr
       eṉpaṉa. ippōrkaḷiṟ cila, iṉṉakāraṇampaṟri nikaḻntaṉa eṉṟa
       vivarattaic ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ cakōtararē viḷakkiṉa āyiṉum muṉṉūlkaḷiṉ
       kuṟippukkoṇṭu aṭiyilvarumāṟu aṟitaṟpālaṉa;
       Page 28
       ippōrkaḷuḷ--
       (1) ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ tāyāṉa naṟcōṇai iṟantapōtu,avaḷporuṭṭuc camaitta
       pattiṉikkallai(cakakamaṉañceyta pattiṉiyiṉuruvam varainta cilai;
       itaṉai, māstikal eṉpar, kaṉṉaṭanāṭṭār.) nīrāṭṭit tūymai ceytaṟkuc
       ceṉṟa kaṅkāyāttiraiyil, āriyavaracar palar tiraṇṭuvantu
       ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉai etirttup perumpōr viḷaittaṉareṉṟum, avaraiyellām
       appērāṟṟaṅ karaiyil iccēraṉ taṉiyaṉākapporutu veṟṟi koṇṭaṉaṉeṉṟum
       terikiṉṟaṉa. itaṉai,
       "kaṅkaip pēryāṟṟuk kaṭumpuṉa ṉīttam
       eṅkō makaḷai yāṭṭiya vannāḷ
       āriya maṉṉa rīraiñ ñūṟṟuvarkku
       orunī yākiya ceruveṅ kōlaṅ
       kaṇviḻittuk kaṇṭatu kaṭuṅkaṭ kūṟṟam." (cilap. 25: 160-64)
       eṉṉum iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ vākkāl aṟika. ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ tantai
       neṭuñcēralātaṉ, muṟkūṟiyapaṭi perunaṟkiḷḷiyuṭaṉ porutu iṟantapōtu,
       avaṉ maṉaiviyum ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ tāyumākiya naṟcōṇai uṭaṉuyirnīttaṉaḷ
       eṉpatu, puṟanāṉūṟṟiṉ 63-m pāṭalāṟ pulappaṭukiṉṟatu.iṅṅaṉam
       cakakamaṉañceyta tāyiṉporuṭṭuc ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ pattiṉikkal amaittaṉaṉ
       pōlum. iṅṅaṉamaṉṟāyiṉ,taṉimaiyāka iṟakkum makaḷirkkup pattiṉikkal
       eṭuttal ciṟavātākum. ivvāṟu, tāyiṉ paṭimattai nīrāṭṭac ceṉṟapōtu,
       kaṅkaikkaraiyil ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ nikaḻttiya ivvariyaceyal avaṉatu
       kaṉṉippōrkaḷuḷ oṉṟākak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu.
       Page 29
       (2) iṉik koṅkar ceṅkaḷattē ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ oru pōr naṭattiyatākat
       terikiṟatu. itaṉil, cōḻapāṇṭiyaruñcērntu etirttaṉareṉṟum, avaratu
       koṭi paṭaikaḷaiyellām kaikkoṇṭu, appōril yāvarum pukaḻattakka
       periya veṟṟiyaic ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ peṟṟaṉaṉeṉṟum cilappatikāraṅ
       kūṟukiṟatu.
       "numpōl vēntar nummō ṭikalik
       koṅkarceṅ kaḷattuk koṭuvarik kayaṟkoṭi
       pakaippuṟattut tantaṉa rāyiṉu māṅkavai
       ticaimuka vēḻattiṉ ceviyakam pukkaṉa." (cilap.25: 152-55)
       eṉakkāṇka. koṭukūr eṉṟa nakaram ikkoṅkar pōrilē ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉāl
       aḻipaṭṭatākum. "koṭukūreṟintu" eṉpatu patiṟṟuppattu. koṭukūr nāṭu
       eṉpatoṉṟu, ippōtu maicūrirājyattiṉ teṉpakutiyākiya paṉṉāṭu
       viṣayattiṉ oru pirivākavum, cēramāṉukkuriya pūmiyākavum iruntateṉṟu
       paḻaiyacāsaṉamoṉṟu(iravitattaṉatu kumāraliṅka cāsaṉam. ikkoṭukūr
       nāṭu cēraṉukkuriyatāyiruntateṉpatu,mēṟpaṭiiravitattaṉ
       eṉṟaciṟṟaracaṉ,cēraṉ anumatipeṟṟup pirāmaṇaṉoruvaṉukku annāṭṭiluḷḷa
       kirāmamoṉṟait tāṉañceytāṉ eṉpataṉāl aṟiyappaṭum.( Indian
       Antiquary.Vol.XVIII, 1889.p.367.)) kūṟukiṟatu. ik koṭukūrnāṭṭiṉ
       talainakarē cēraṉāl aḻikkappaṭṭatātal vēṇṭum.
       (3). iṉip paraṇareṉṟa pulavar perumāṉ pāṭiya aintāmpattil
       mikutiyākap pārāṭṭappaṭum ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ vīracceyalkaḷ, avaṉ kaṭalil
       cainniyaṅkaḷaic celuttiyatum paḻaiyaṉeṉpavaṉai aḻittatumām.
       Page 30
       ivaṟṟuḷ mutaṟceyal, kaṭalai araṇākakkoṇṭu iṭarviḷaitta
       pakaiyaracarai, eṇṇiṟanta marakkalaṅkaḷmūlam paṭaiyeṭuttuc ceṉṟu
       veṟṟikoṇṭataik kuṟikkiṉṟatu.
       "iṉiyā ruḷarō muṉṉu millai
       vayaṅkumaṇi yimaippiṉ vēliṭupu
       muḻaṅkutiraippaṉikkaṭal maṟuttici ṉōrē." (45)
       " kōṭunaral pavvaṅ kalaṅka vēliṭ
       ṭuṭaitiraip parappiṟ paṭukaṭa lōṭṭiya
       velpukaḻkkuṭṭuvaṉ. " (46)
       "keṭalarum palpukaḻ nilaii nīrpukkuk
       kaṭalo ṭuḻanta paṉittuṟaip paratava." (48)
       eṉap patiṟṟuppattiṉum,
       "paṭainilā vilaṅkuṅ kaṭaṉmaru ṭāṉai
       maṭṭaviḻ teriyaṉ maṟappōrk kuṭṭuvaṉ
       porumuraṇ poṟātu vilaṅkuciṉañciṟantu
       ceruccey muṉpoṭu munnīr muṟṟi
       ōṅkutiraip pauva nīṅka vōṭṭiya
       nīrmā ṇeḵka niṟuttucceṉ ṟaḻuntak
       kūrmata ṉaḻiyarō neñcē. " (212)
       eṉa akanāṉūṟṟiṉum varum paraṇar vākkukkaḷāl, ceytaṟkariya
       kaṭaṟpōroṉṟu ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉāl nikaḻttappaṭṭamai teḷiyappaṭum.
       iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ,
       "neṭaṅkaṭa lōṭṭi, uṭaṟṟumēl vanta vāriyamaṉṉaraik
       kaṭumpuṉaṟ kaṅkaip pēryāṟṟu veṉṟōy. " (28;119-21)
       Page 31
       "maṟattuṟai muṭitta vāyvāṭ ṭāṉaiyoṭu
       poṅkirum parappiṟ kaṭalpiṟak kōṭṭik
       kaṅkaip pēryāṟ ṟukkarai pōkiya
       ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ." (cilap. iṟutikkaṭṭurai.)
       eṉa, kaṭalōṭṭiya ceytiyōṭucērttu āriyarai veṟṟi koṇṭatai irumuṟai
       iṇaittuk kūṟutalāl, ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ, taṉ tāyiṉ paṭimattai nīrāṭṭutaṟkuk
       kaṅkāyāttirai ceytapōtu, iṅṅaṉam pakai veṉṟavaṉākavē tōṟṟukiṟatu.
       kaṭaliṟceyta ivvaruñceyalpaṟṟiyē "cēramāṉ kaṭalōṭṭiya vēl
       keḻukuṭṭuvaṉ" (puṟam-369.) "kaṭalpiṟak kōṭṭiya
       ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ"(patiṟṟuppattu 5-m pattuppatikam) eṉa ivvēntaṉ
       pukaḻappaṭukiṉṟāṉ.ivaṉatu kaṭaṟpaṭaiyeṭuppil, taṉakkuc ciṟanta
       vēlaiyēṟṟik kalaṅkaḷaic celutti, taṉṉuḷ vāḻvārkku araṇāyirunta
       kaṭalvaliyai aḻittamaiyāl, mēṟkuṟitta peyarkaḷ vaḻaṅkalāyiṉa
       eṉka.(patiṟṟuppattu 5-m pattuppatikam 45, 46, urai kāṇka.) ivaṉ
       tantai neṭuñcēralātaṉum iṅṅaṉamē kaṭaṉaṭuvilirunta taṉ pakaivar mēṟ
       paṭaiyeṭuttuc ceṉṟu veṉṟa ceyti muṉṉarē kuṟikkappaṭṭatu.(innūl vēṟu
       pakkaṅkāṇka) iṉi, iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ,
       "koṅkaṇar kaliṅkar koṭuṅkaru nāṭar
       paṅkaḷar kaṅkar palvēṟ kaṭṭiyar
       vaṭavā rīyaroṭu vaṇṭamiḻ mayakkattuṉ
       kaṭamalai vēṭṭameṉ kaṭpulam piriyātu." (cilap. 25:156-59)
       eṉṟu kūṟiya ceyti, ippōruṭaṉ toṭarpuṭaiyatō aṉṟit taṉiyāṉatō
       terintilatu.
       Page 32
       itaṉāl, taṉ kālattaracar palarkkup pēraccamviḷaitta peruvīraṉāka
       viḷaṅkiyavaṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ eṉpatu peṟappaṭum.
       (4) iṉic ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉatu veṟṟippukaḻk kētuvākiya maṟṟoru pōr,
       paḻaiyaṉ eṉpāṉuṭaṉ nikaḻntatākum.paḻaiyaṉ eṉpavaṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ
       paṭaittalaivaṉākiya peruvīraṉ. ivaṉ mōkūr eṉṉum ūrukkuriyavaṉ;
       "paḻaiyaṉ mōkū ravaiyakam" eṉpatu maturaikkāñci. ippaḻaiyaṉai
       "mōkūr" eṉavum vaḻaṅkuvar;"moyvaḷañ cerukki mocintuvaru mōkūr"
       (patiṟṟuppattu.44)"mōkūr paṇiyāmaiyiṉ" (akam. 251) eṉak kāṇka.
       mōriyavaracar tikkuvicayañ ceytukoṇṭu teṉṟicai nōkki vantapōtu,
       ippaḻaiyaṉ avarkkup paṇiyāmaiyāl, avarkkum ivaṉukkum potiyamalaip
       pakkattē pōr nikaḻntateṉṟu terikiṟatu. ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ ippaḻaiyaṉuṭaṉ
       pakaimai pūṇṭu pōrpurintatu,neṭuntūrattirunta taṉ naṭparacaṉākiya
       aṟukai eṉpavaṉukku ippaḻaiyaṉ pakaivaṉāyiruntamaiyāl, an naṇpaṉukku
       utavi ceyvaṟkākavē eṉpatu,
       "nuṇkoṭi yuḻiñai velpō raṟukai
       cēṇa ṉāyiṉuṅ kēḷeṉa moḻintu
       pulampeyarn toḷitta kaḷaiyāp pūcaṟ
       karaṇkaṭā vuṟīi yaṇaṅku nikaḻntaṉṉa
       mōkūr maṉṉaṉ muracaṅ koṇṭu."
       eṉṉum paraṇarvākkāl aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu. īṇṭu aṟukaiyeṉappaṭṭavaṉ
       mōriyavamicattutitta aracaṉ pōlum.ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ nikaḻttiya
       ipperumpōril, paḻaiyaṉatu.
       Page 33
       kāvaṉ maramākiya vēmpiṉait tuṇṭantuṇṭaṅkaḷākat taṟippittu,
       appaḻaiyaṉ yāṉaikaḷaiyē kaṭāvākavum, avaṉ makaḷiratu kūntalai
       aṟuttuttirittu ataṉaiy kayiṟākavuṅ koṇṭu vaṇṭiyiliḻuppittaṉaṉ eṉak
       kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. (patiṟṟuppattu. 44) ippōraippaṟṟi,
       iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum,
       "paḻaiyaṉ kākkuṅ kuḻaipayi ṉeṭuṅkōṭṭu
       vēmpumutal taṭinta ēntuvāḷ valattup
       pōntaik kaṇṇip poṟaiya" ( cilap.27.124-26.)
       eṉṟu kūṟiṉār.
       (5) iṉi, kaṭaṟkaraiyiluḷḷa viyalūrai orupōriṟ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ
       eṟintaṉaṉeṉṟum, itaṉaiyaṭuttu nērivāyiliṟ cōḻamaṉṉaroṉpatiṉmarai
       aḻittatōṭu, iṭumpātavaṉattum (itu, cōḻanāṭṭil tiruttuṟaippūṇṭiyai
       aṭuttuḷḷatum, tēvārappāṭal peṟṟatumāṉa iṭumpāvaṉam eṉṟa talam
       pōlum.) veṟṟi peṟṟaṉaṉeṉṟum cilappatikāraṅkuṟikkiṉṟatu.
       "ciṟukura ṉeytal viyalū reṟintapiṉ
       ārpuṉai teriya loṉpatu maṉṉarai
       nērivāyil nilaic ceru veṉṟu
       neṭuntērt tāṉaiyo ṭiṭumpiṟ puṟattiṟuttuk
       koṭumpōr kaṭantu." (cilap.28:115-19.)
       ivaṟṟuḷ oṉpatumaṉṉareṉṟatu,cōḻarkuṭiyiṟ piṟanta iḷaṅkōvēntar
       oṉpatiṉamarai. ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉukku ammāṉākiya cōḻaṉ iṟantatum,
       avaṉmakaṉum taṉ maittuṉaṉumākiya, iḷañcōḻaṉ paṭṭattai aṭainta
       kālattē, atupoṟāmal peruṅkalakam viḷaittuc cōṇāṭṭai alaittu niṉṟa
       cōḻavamicattavar oṉpatiṉmarai uṟaiyūrkkut teṉpakkattuḷḷa nērivāyil
       (nērivāyil – uṟaiyūrt teṟkil vāyilatōr ūr' eṉṟār.cilappatikāra
       arumpatavuraikārar (pak-73).
       Page 34
       ippeyar koṇṭa ūr, uṟaiyūraic cūḻnta nāṭṭukkup piṟkālattu vaḻaṅkiya
       peyarākiya kṣattiriya cikāmaṇi vaḷanāṭṭaic cārntirunta teṉpatu,
       "kṣattiriyacikāmaṇi vaḷanāṭṭup paṉaiyūrnāṭṭu nērvāyiluṭaiyāṉ...
       vāṉavaṉ pallava taraiyaṉ" eṉṉum cāsaṉappakutiyāl viḷaṅkum. (South
       Indian Inscriptions. Vol.III.No.21)) eṉṟa ūril nikaḻnta pōrilē
       orupakaliṟkoṉṟu, taṉ maittuṉac cōḻaṉaip paṭṭattil niṟuviṉaṉ
       ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ. taṉ tamaiyaṉatu ivvariyaceyalai viyantu:-
       "maittuṉa vaḷavaṉ kiḷḷiyoṭu poruntā
       otta paṇpiṉa roṉpatu maṉṉar
       iḷavaracu poṟāar ēval kēḷār
       vaḷanā ṭaḻikku māṇpiṉa rātaliṉ
       oṉpatu kuṭaiyu morupaka loḻittavaṉ
       poṉpuṉai tikiri oruvaḻip paṭuttōy." (cilap.27:118-23)
       eṉa iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷē kūṟutal kāṇka. "ārāt tiruviṟ cōḻarkuṭik kuriyōr
       - oṉpatiṉmar vīḻa vāyiṟpuṟattiṟuttu" eṉṟār patiṟṟuppattiṉum.
       ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ maittuṉaṉākiya ivviḷañcōḻaṉ peyar peruṅkiḷḷi yeṉpatu
       cilappatikārattu uraipeṟu kaṭṭuraiyāṟ pulappaṭum.(ivaṉaip
       perunaṟkiḷḷi eṉpar, aṭiyārkku nallār.(cilap.pak.32)) iṉṉum itaṉ
       virivai 'ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ kālattaracar' eṉṟa pakutiyiṟ kāṇka. iv
       varalāṟukaḷāl,ceṅkuṭṭuva ṉikaḻttiya pōrkaḷiṟcila potuvāka oruvāṟu
       aṟiyappaṭutal kaṇṭukoḷḷalām.
       --------------------------------
       page 35
       4-ām atikāram:-
       ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ kālattu iraṇṭu carita nikaḻccikaḷ
       ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉatu nīṭitta āṭcikkālattil nikaḻnta vicēṭaṅkaḷuḷḷē,
       iraṇṭu caritaṅkaḷ mukkiyamāṉavai. avai, kōvalaṉ kaṇṇakikaḷaip
       paṟṟiyatum, kōvalaṉuṭaiya kaṇikaimakaḷ maṇimēkalaiyaip
       paṟṟiyatumām. ivviruvar caritaṅkaḷil, muṉṉataic ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ
       cakōtararāṉa iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ cilappatikārameṉṟa kāviyattālum,
       piṉṉatai avvaṭikaḷ kālattup pulavarākiya maturaik kūla vāṇikaṉ
       cāttaṉār maṇimēkalaiyeṉṟa kāviyattālum mika vaḻakākap
       pāṭiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. ivviraṇṭu nūlkaḷum akavaṟpāvil taṉittaṉi muppatu
       kātaikaḷuṭaiyaṉavāka amaintuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟiṟkaṇṭa avviruvar
       carittirac curukkaṅkaḷum aṭiyil varumāṟu:-
       I. kōvalaṉ kaṇṇakikaḷ varalāṟu
       cōṇāṭṭil, kāviri kaṭaloṭu kalakkumiṭattamaintatum cōḻaratu paḻaiya
       irājatāṉiyumākiya pukār eṉṟa kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattil, vaṇikar
       talaivaṉākiya mācāttuvāṉ eṉpavaṉ taṉmakaṉ kōvalaṉukku, mānāykaṉ
       eṉṟa vaṇikaṉ makaḷ kaṇṇakiyai maṇampurivittut taṉimāḷikaiyil
       avarkaḷai vāḻntu varumpaṭi ceyya, avarkaḷum aṟavōrkkaḷittal
       anataṇarōmpal mutaliya tarumaṅkaḷaic ceytu illaṟattai iṉitu naṭatti
       vantaṉar.
       Page 36
       iṅṅaṉam nikaḻntuvaruṅkāl, mikka aḻakum āṭal pāṭalkaḷiṟ
       ṟērcciyumuṭaiya mātavi eṉṉum nāṭakak kaṇikaiyaik kōvalaṉ kātaluṟṟu,
       taṉpāluḷḷa poruḷkaḷai nāḷum avaḷ poruṭṭuc celaviṭṭu avaḷuṭaṉ
       makiḻntu varuvāṉāyiṉaṉ. kaṇṇaki taṉ kaṇavaṉatu pirivukku
       varuntiṉaḷāyiṉum, ataṉai veḷikkāṭṭātiruntāḷ. iṅṅaṉam nikaḻa,
       cōḻarkaḷ intiraṉ poruṭṭu āṇṭutōṟum naṭattum intira viḻāvāṉatu
       akkālattu naṭaipeṟṟatu. ataṉmuṭivil nakarattuḷḷār, vaḻakkampōlat
       tattam parivāraṅkaḷuṭaṉ kaṭalāṭutaṟkuc ceṉṟaṉar. kōvalaṉum
       mātaviyuṭaṉ kaṭaṟkaraiyaṭaintu ōriṭattiruntu palavakaip pāṭalḷaip
       pāṭikkoṇṭu vīṇaiyai eṭuttu vācittāṉ. avaṉ pāṭiyavai, vevvēṟu
       akapporuṭcuvai taḻuviyiruntamaiyāl, mātavi, ' ivaṉ vēṟu makaḷirpāl
       viruppuṭaiyaṉ pōlum' eṉṟeṇṇippulantu, avaṉ kaiyāḻai vāṅkit, tāṉ
       vēṟukuṟippillātavaḷāyiṉum, ak kuṟippukkoṇṭa akapporuṭcuvai taḻuviya
       pāṭṭukkaḷaip pāṭikkoṇṭu vīṇaiyai vācittaṉaḷ. avaḷ pāṭiyavaṟṟaik
       kēṭṭirunta kōvalaṉ ' vēṟoruvaṉmēṟ kātalkoṇṭu ivaḷ pāṭiṉaḷ' eṉṟeṇṇi,
       ūḻvacattāl avaḷait tuṟantu taṉ maṉaiyaṭaintu kaṇṇakiyaik kaṇṭu, "
       poyyai meyyākak kāṭṭiyoḻukum parattaiyai maruvi vaṟumaiyuṟṟuk
       keṭṭēṉ; aḵtu eṉakku mikavum nāṇaittarukiṉṟatu" eṉṟāṉ.
       Page 37
       kaṇṇaki, ' mātavikku mēluṅkoṭukkap poruḷiṉmaiyāl iṅṅaṉam
       kavalkiṉṟāṉ' eṉṟu karuti,' aṭiyēṉiṭattu iraṇṭu cilampukaḷ uḷḷaṉa:
       avaṟṟaik koṇṭaruḷka' eṉṟāḷ. itukēṭṭa kōvalaṉ, ' āyiṉ
       maturaiyaṭaintu accilampaiyē mutalākakkoṇṭu vāṇikañceytu poruḷtēṭa
       eṇṇukiṉṟēṉ; nīyum uṭaṉvaruka' eṉṟāṉ.itaṟkuk kaṇṇakiyum
       cammatikka,avviraviṉ kaṭaiyāmattē oruvarumaṟiyāvakai avviruvarum
       puṟappaṭṭuk kāviriyiṉ vaṭakarai vaḻiyē ceṉṟu,āṅkoru cōlaiyil
       maturaikkup puṟappaṭac cittamāyirunta kauntiyaṭikaḷ eṉṟa
       jainacanyāsiniyuṭaṉ kūṭippirayāṇañ ceytukoṇṭu uṟaiyūr vantu
       cērntārkaḷ.
       iṅṅaṉam vantavarkaḷ, maṟunāḷ avvūriṉiṉṟu puṟappaṭṭu
       iṭaivaḻiyiletirppaṭṭa antaṇaṉoruvaṉāl maturaikku mārkkan
       terintukoṇṭu appāṟ ceṉṟaṉar. celkaiyil, kālaikkaṭaṉ kaḻippataṟkākak
       kōvalaṉ oru poykaikkaraiyai aṭaintuniṟka, āṅkut taṉ nāṭakak
       kaṇikaiyāl viṭukkappaṭṭut taṉṉait tēṭivanta kaucikaṉ eṉṟa
       pārppaṉaṉaikkaṇṭu, taṉ tantaitāyariṉ tuyaraṅkaḷaiyum mātaviyiṉ
       pirivāṟṟāmaiyaiyum avaṉ collakkēṭṭu varunti, tāṉ puṟappaṭṭa
       kāraṇattait taṉ peṟṟōrkkukkūṟit taṉ vantaṉaṅkaḷaiyum terivikkumpaṭi
       colli, avaṉai yaṉuppiviṭṭu, muṉ pōlavē kauntiyaṭikaḷuṭaṉ
       pirayāṇamāki maturaiyai neruṅki, vaiyaiyāṟṟait toḻutu paṭakēṟi,
       annakariṉ matiṟpuṟattuḷḷa jaiṉamuṉivarirukkaiyil avvaṭikaḷuṭaṉ
       taṅkiṉāṉ.
       Page 38
       maṟunāṭkālaiyil, kōvalaṉ kauntiyaṭikaḷai vaṇaṅkit taṉ
       tuṉpaṅkaḷaikkūṟi varuttamaṭaiya, avaḷāl tēṟṟappaṭṭu, kaṇṇakiyai
       avvaṭikaḷ pārvaiyil vaittuppiṉ, vāṇikañceytaṟkuriya iṭamaṟintu
       varumporuṭṭu maturaiyiṉuḷḷē piravēcittu an nakaravaḷaṅkaḷaikkaṇṭu
       makiḻntu tirumpik kauntiyiṭam vantu cērntu, taṉpaḻaiya naṭpāḷaṉum
       talaicceṅkāṉam eṉṉum ūriluḷḷavaṉumākiya māṭalaṉeṉṉum antaṇaṉai
       avviṭattukkaṇṭu aḷavaḷāvik koṇṭiruntāṉ. appōtu
       kauntiyaṭikaḷ,tammiṭamvanta iṭaicciyar talaiviyākiya mātari
       eṉpavaḷatu uttamakuṇaṅkaḷai nōkki, avaḷpāṟ kaṇṇakiyai aṭaikkalamāka
       oppikka, avaḷ makiḻcciyuṭaṉēṟṟuk kaṇṇakiyaik kōvalaṉuṭaṉ
       aḻaittukkoṇṭu āyarpāṭiyiluḷḷa taṉ maṉaiyoṉṟil avarkaḷaiyamartti,
       camaittuṇṇutaṟku vēṇṭiya paṇṭaṅkaḷumaḷittut taṉ makaḷ aiyaiyaik
       kaṇṇakikkut tuṇaiyākavaittup peritum upacarittaṉaḷ. kaṇṇakiyum tāṉ
       peṟṟavaṟṟaip pakkuvamākac camaittut taṉ kaṇavaṉukku muṟaippaṭi
       parimāṟak,kōvalaṉ iṉitāka uṇṭu, piṉ taṉ maṉaiviyai arukilaḻaittut
       tāṉ avaṭkucceyta tavaṟukaḷaiyum taṉ kūṭāvoḻukkaṅkaḷaiyum kūṟi muṉ
       purintavaṟṟukku iraṅki avaḷai yarumaipārāṭṭi viṭṭu, 'iṉi uṉ
       cilampukaḷil oṉṟaikkoṇṭu nakarattuḷḷē ceṉṟu viṟṟu viraivil varuvaṉ;
       atuvaraiyil nī āṟṟiyiru;' eṉṟucolli,avaḷatu cilampukaḷiloṉṟai
       vāṅkikkoṇṭu ceṉṟu,etirppaṭṭa tuṉṉimittaṅkaḷaiyum aṟiyātavaṉāy
       maturaiyiṉ āvaṇavītiyiṉuḷḷē pukuntaṉaṉ.
       Page 39
       iṅṅaṉam pukutalum, poṟkollarnūṟṟuvar taṉ piṉ vara mikka
       āṭamparattuṭaṉ
       varukiṉṟa poṟkollar talaivaṉaik kōvalaṉ kaṇṭu, 'ivaṉ aracaṉāṟ
       peyarpeṟṟavaṉpōlum' eṉṟeṇṇi avaṉarukiṟceṉṟu, "aracaṉtēvi aṇitaṟkut
       takutiyāṉa cilampoṉṟai vilai matittaṟku nī takutiyuṭaiyaiyō" eṉṟu
       avaṉai yucāva,akkollaṉum mikkapaṇivuṭaṉ tāṉ vallaṉātalaik
       kuṟippikka kōvalaṉ tāṉkoṇarnta cilampiṉai avaṉiṭaṅ kāṭṭiṉāṉ. avaṉ
       pārttu, 'itaṉai aracaṉukku nāṉ terivittu varumaḷavum ivviṭattē
       nīvir irum' eṉṟu ōriṭattaik kōvalaṉukkuk kāṭṭic ceṉṟāṉ. ceṉṟa
       poṟkollaṉ, muṉpu aracaṉmaṉaiyuḷ cilampoṉṟai vañcittut
       tiruṭikkoṇṭavaṉātalāl, 'yāṉ kavarnta cilampu eṉṉiṭattēyuḷḷatu eṉṟu
       aracaṉ aṟitaṟkumuṉṉē ataṉōṭotta cilampaik koṇarnta ipputiyavaṉāl
       eṉmītuṇṭākum aiyattaip pōkkikkoḷvēṉ'eṉṟu taṉakkuḷḷē cūḻntu,
       araṇmaṉaiyai-yaṭaintu, kāmaparavacaṉāy antappurattai nōkkic
       ceṉṟukoṇṭirunta pāṇṭiyaṉaikkaṇṭu vaṇaṅki - 'aracē! kaṉṉakkōl
       mutaliyavaiyillāmalē araṇmaṉaiyuḷḷirunta cilampait tiruṭiyakaḷvaṉ
       aṭiyēṉuṭaiyamaṉaiyil vantirukkiṟāṉ.' eṉṟu colla, pāṇṭiyaṉ uṭaṉē
       kāvalāḷarai aḻaittu,'eṉ maṉaiviyiṉ cilampu ivaṉkūṟiya kaḷvaṉ
       kaiyiṭattatāyiṉ avaṉaik koṉṟu accilampaik koṇarutir' eṉṟu
       ārāycciyiṉṟiyē kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭṭaṉaṉ. poṟkollaṉ taṉateṇṇam palittateṉṟu
       akamakiḻntu akkāvalāḷarkaḷuṭaṉ ceṉṟu kōvalaṉaiyaṭaintu avaṉai
       nōkki, 'ivarkaḷ aracaṉ kaṭṭaḷaippaṭi cilampukāṇa vantavarkaḷ' eṉṟu
       kūṟavum, kōvalaṉ mukakkuṟi mutaliyavaṟṟaik kaṇṭu 'ivaṉ kaḷvaṉallaṉ'
       eṉṟu kūṟiya kāvalāḷarkku 'ivaṉ kaḷvaṉē' eṉpatai vaṟpuṟuttip
       pakkattil niṉṟāṉ. appōtu, avarkaḷiṟ kolaiyañ cātāṉoruvaṉ viraintu
       ceṉṟu taṉ kaivāḷāṟ kōvalaṉai veṭṭi vīḻttiṉaṉ.
       Page 40
       ippāl, kaṇṇakiyirunta iṭaiccēriyilē palavita uṟpātaṅkaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa.
       avaṟṟaikkaṇṭa mātari mutaliyōrāl uṟpāta cāntiyākat
       tirumālaikkuṟittuk kuravaik kūttoṉṟu nikaḻttappaṭṭatu. ataṉ muṭivil
       mātari nīrāṭutaṟ poruṭṭu vaiyaiyāṟṟukkuc ceṉṟāḷ.appoḻutu, cilampu
       tiruṭiyavaṉeṉṟu kōvalaṉaik kāvalāḷar koṉṟa ceytiyai
       maturaiyuḷḷiruntuvanta orutti collakkēṭṭa kaṇṇaki, pataipataittu
       mūrccittup palavāṟu pulampit tāṉum avaṉuṭaṉ iṟakkat tuṇintu,
       iṭaicciyarmattiyil niṉṟu, cūriyaṉai nōkki 'ceṅkatirc celvaṉē! nī
       yaṟiya eṉ kaṇavaṉ kaḷvaṉō?'eṉṟāḷ. avaṉ, 'niṉ kaṇavaṉ kaḷvaṉallaṉ;
       avaṉai avvāṟucoṉṉa ivvūrai viraivil tīyuṇṇum' eṉṟu acarīriyākak
       kūṟiṉaṉ. ataṉaikkēṭṭa kaṇṇaki mikunta cīṟṟattōṭum taṉṉiṭamuḷḷa
       maṟṟorucilampuṭaṉē puṟappaṭṭuk kaṇṭārnaṭunaṭuṅkumpaṭi
       vītivaḻiyēceṉṟu aṅku niṉṟa makaḷirai nōkkip palavāṟu pulampi
       veṭṭuṇṭu kiṭanta kōvalaṉaic cilar kāṭṭakkaṇṭu aḷavukaṭanta
       tuṉpattilāḻntu avaṉai muṉṉilaippaṭuttip palavāṟu piralāpittu
       avaṉuṭampait taḻuvik koḷḷavum, avvaḷavil
       Page 41
       avaṉ uyirpeṟṟeḻuntu 'matipōṉṟa mukam vāṭiyatē' eṉṟu collit taṉ
       kaiyālē avaḷ kaṇṇīrai māṟṟa, kaṇṇaki avaṉ pātaṅkaḷaip paṇintaṉaḷ.
       uṭaṉē, avaṉ 'nī iṅkiru' eṉṟu colli avvuṭampai oḻittuviṭṭut
       tēvarulakam pukuntāṉ.
       iṅkē ivvāṟu nikaḻap, pāṇṭiyaṉ maṉaivi tāṉ kaṇṭa tīccakuṉaṅkaḷait
       taṉ kaṇavaṉuṭaṉ pēcikkoṇṭirukku nilaiyilē, kaṇṇaki kōpamikkavaḷāy
       araṇmaṉai yaṭaintu, vāyilkāvalaṉāl taṉ varavai aracaṉukkaṟivittu,
       anumatipeṟṟu aracaṉmuṉceṉṟu, avaṉkēṭpat taṉ ūr peyar
       mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum, ārāyātu kōvalaṉaik kolvitta avaṉatu
       koṭuṅkōṉmaiyaiyum mikunta
       tuṇivuṭaṉ eṭuttu moḻintaṉaḷ. itaṉaikkēṭṭa pāṇṭiyaṉ 'kaḷvaṉaik kōṟal
       kaṭuṅkōl aṉṟu – vēḷvēṟ koṟṟaṅkāṇ' eṉṟu kūṟa, kaṇṇaki taṉ kaṇavaṉ
       kaḷvaṉallaṉ eṉpataik kāṭṭum poruṭṭut 'taṉ cilampiṉuḷḷēyirukkum
       paral māṇikkamām' eṉṟāḷ. itukēṭṭa aracaṉ 'naṉṟu; tēvicilampiṉ paral
       muttu' eṉṟāṉ. uṭaṉē, avaṟṟiṉ uṇmaiyaic cōtippataṟkākak
       kōvalaṉiṭamirunta cilampu varuvikkappaṭṭatu. ataṉaik kaṇṇaki vāṅkit
       taṉkaiyāl uṭaikkalāṉāḷ. uṭaikkavum ataṉuḷḷirunta māṇikkapparal
       pāṇṭiyaṉatu
       vāyaṭiyiṟceṉṟu teṟittatu. atukaṇṭu aracaṉ patai pataittu - "iḻinta
       poṟkollaṉ collaik kēṭṭa koṭuṅkōlaṉākiya yāṉō aracaṉ; yāṉē kaḷvaṉ;
       antō! mikap pukaḻpaṭaitta ivvarumanta kulam eṉṉāṟ perum
       paḻiyaṭaintatē; eṉ āyuḷ ippōtē aḻiyakkaṭavatāka" eṉṟukūṟip pulampi
       maṉamuṭaintu tāṉirunta ācaṉattē vīḻntu uyirviṭṭaṉaṉ. taṉ
       kaṇavaṉiṟantaceyti yaṟinta cilanērattukkuḷ, attukka māṟṟātu avaṉ
       kōpperuntēviyum uyir nīṅkiṉaḷ.
       Page 42
       iṅṅaṉam, kaṇṇaki, taṉ kaṇavaṉ kaḷvaṉallaṉ eṉpataip pāṇṭiyaṉmuṉ
       vaḻakkāṭi meyppittu, muṉkoṇṭa kōpañ ciṟitun taṇiyāḷāy, 'nāṉ
       pattiṉiyā
       yiruppatu uṇmaiyāyiṉ ivvūrai aḻippēṉ' eṉṟu capatañ ceytu koṇṭu
       araṇmaṉaiyaiviṭṭup puṟappaṭṭāḷ. puṟappaṭṭu, maturaiyiṉuḷḷē
       piravēcittut taṉ iṭakkoṅkaiyait tiruki yeṭuttu ataṉai annakariṉ
       mīteṟiya, avaḷ colliyavaṇṇamē, annakaruḷ tīppaṟṟik koṇṭu
       palaviṭaṅkaḷaiyum erittatu. ataṉ vemmaiyai āṟṟāta maturaiyiṉ
       atitēvataiyāṉavaḷ kaṇṇakimuṉ vantu niṉṟu, avaḷai nōkki, 'yāṉ
       innakariṉ teyvam; uṉakkuc cilavuṇmai kūṟa vantēṉ; avaṟṟaik
       kēṭpāyāka; innakarattil muṉpirunta pāṇṭiyarkaḷuḷ
       oruvarēṉuñ ciṟituṅ koṭuṅkōṉmai yuṭaiyavarallar. inneṭuñceḻiyaṉum at
       taṉmaiyaṉēyāvaṉ; āyiṉum ittuṉpam uṉakku vantavaralāṟṟaik kūṟuvēṉ;
       muṉpu kaliṅkanāṭṭuc ciṅkapurattaracaṉākiya vacu eṉpavaṉum, kapila
       purattaracaṉākiya kumāṉeṉapavaṉum tammuṭ pakaikoṇṭu oruvaraiyoruvar
       vellak karutiyiruntaṉar.
       Page 43
       appōtu ciṅkapurattuk kaṭaivītiyiṟ paṇṭam viṟṟuk koṇṭirunta
       caṅkamaṉeṉṉum vaṇikaṉai, an nakarattaracaṉiṭam toḻil
       ceytukoṇṭirunta parataṉeṉpavaṉ, ivaṉ pakaivaṉatu oṟṟaṉeṉṟupiṭittu
       aracaṉukkukkāṭṭi avaṉaik kolai ceytuviṭṭāṉ. appōtu ac caṅkamaṉ
       maṉaiviyākiya nīliyeṉpavaḷ mikunta tuyaramuṟṟu patiṉāṉkunāḷ
       palaviṭattum alaintu, piṉpu orumalaiyiṉmītēṟik kaṇavaṉaic
       cērtaṟporuṭṭut taṉṉuyirai viṭa niṉaintavaḷ,'emakkut tuṉpamviḷaittōr
       maṟupiṟappil ittuṉpattaiyē aṭaivārkaḷāka' eṉṟu cāpamiṭṭiṟantaṉaḷ.
       ap parataṉē kōvalaṉākap piṟantāṉ. ātalāl nīṅkaḷ it tuṉpam
       aṭaintīrkaḷ. iṟṟaikkup patiṉāṉkāvatu nāḷil pakal ceṉṟa piṉpu, nī uṉ
       kaṇavaṉaikkaṇṭu cērvāy" eṉṟu colli avaḷaittēṟṟi maturāpatiyākiya
       atteyvañ ceṉṟatu. piṉpu kaṇṇaki maturaiyai viṭṭu nīṅki, vaiyaik
       karaivaḻiyē mēṟṟicai nōkkic ceṉṟu malaināṭaṭaintu ceṅkuṉṟeṉṉum
       malaiyiṉmēlēṟi oru vēṅkaimarattu niḻalilniṟka, patiṉāṉkāntiṉattup
       pakalceṉṟapiṉpu, aṅkē teyvavaṭivōṭu vanta kōvalaṉaik kaṇṭu kaḷittu
       avaṉuṭaṉ vimāṉamēṟit tēvarkaḷ pōṟṟumpaṭi viṇṇulakaṭaintāḷ.
       ivvaḷavē, cilappatikārattiṉ maturaippukārk kāṇṭaṅkaḷiṟ ciṟappittuk
       kūṟappaṭuvatu. mūṉṟāvatāṉa vañcikkāṇṭameṉṉum pakutiyil, kaṇṇaki
       viṇṇulakañ ceṉṟataik kaṇṇārakkaṇṭa vēṭuvarkaḷ tiraṇṭu, av
       vicēṭattait tammaracaṉākiya ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉukkut terivittatu mutaliya
       ceytikaḷ vivarikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟaic 'ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ
       vaṭayāttirai'yiṟ kūṟuvōm.

       page 44
       II. maṇimēkalai varalāṟu.
       kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉattē peruṅkuṭivāṇikar marapilutitta kōvalaṉukku,
       mātaviyeṉṉum nāṭakakkaṇikaiyiṭam maṇimēkalai eṉpavaḷ
       piṟantiruntaṉaḷ. ivaḷ tāyākiya mātavi, taṉ kātalaṉāṉa kōvalaṉ
       maturaiyiṟ kolai-yuṇṭiṟantatu kēṭṭut taṉ kulattoḻilai muṟṟum
       veṟuttup pauttamuṉivarākiya aṟavaṇavaṭikaḷaic caraṇamaṭaintu avarāl
       vāymai nāṉkuñ cīlamaintum upatēcikkappeṟṟup pauttacaṅkattaic cērntu
       pikṣuṇiyāyiṉaḷ. avaḷmakaḷ maṇimēkalaiyō, taṉ tāyuṭaṉ paḻakivantatāṟ
       ciṟupirāyattē pauttatarumaṅkaḷai aṟitaṟkēṟṟa uṇarccipeṟṟiruntāḷ.
       orunāḷ maṇimēkalai tāyiṉ kaṭṭaḷaippaṭi taṉtōḻiyākiya cutamatiyuṭaṉ
       pūkkoytu varuvataṟku upavaṉañcella, āṅkut taṉṉai virumpivanta
       cōḻaṉ-kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ makaṉākiya utayakumaraṉukku añciyavaḷāy,
       āṅkirunta paḷikkaṟaiyoṉṟiṟ patuṅkiyiruntu avaṉ pōyiṉapiṉpu veḷiyē
       vantāḷ.piṉṉar, avaḷ kulatēvataiyāṉa maṇimēkalā teyvam tōṉṟi,
       maṇipallavam eṉṉuntīviṟku avaḷai aḻaittuk koṇṭu pōyiṟṟu.
       attīviṟceṉṟa maṇimēkalai aṅkuḷḷa puttapīṭikaikkāṭciyāl taṉ
       paḻampiṟappil nikaḻnta vicēṭaṅkaḷai aṟintatōṭu, atteyvam
       aṟivuṟuttiya mūṉṟu mantiraṅkaḷai uṇarntu, muṟpiṟappil taṉ
       kaṇavaṉākavirunta irākulaṉ eṉpavaṉē ippiṟappil utayakumaraṉāka
       vantāṉ eṉpataiyum attēvataiyāl aṟintaṉaḷ.
       Page 45
       piṉṉar, appīṭikaiyiṉ kāvaṟṟeyvamāṉa tīvatilakaiyiṉutaviyāl
       aṅkēyuḷḷa kōmukiyeṉṉum poykaiyai aṭaintu, atilirunta amutacurapi
       eṉṉum akṣaya pāttiram taṉ kaiyil varappeṟṟu, appāttirattuṭaṉ taṉ
       nakarākiya pukāraiyaṭaintu, aṟavaṇavaṭikaḷiṭañ ceṉṟu nikaḻntatu
       kūṟi vaṇaṅkiṉāḷ. ammuṉivar, āputtiraṉ varalāṟṟaiyum, avaṉukku
       maturaiyiṟ cintātēvi yaḷitta
       amutacurapiyē avaḷkaip pukuntataiyum, appāttirattāṟ
       pacittavuyirkaṭku uṇavaḷittaliṉ ciṟappaiyum maṇimēkalaikku
       uṇarttiṉar. avar kūṟiyavāṟē, uṇavaḷittaṟ poruṭṭu, tāṉpeṟṟa
       amutacurapiyaik kaiyilēntiṉavaḷāy maṇimēkalai vītiyiṟcella, avaḷatu
       akṣaya pāttirattil uttama pattiṉiyākiya ātiraiyeṉpavaḷ vantu
       mutalil piccaiyiṭṭaṉaḷ.
       piṉpu maṇimēkalai ap pāttirattiṉiṉṟu eṭuttutaviya orupiṭiyamutāl,
       kāyacaṇṭikai yeṉṉum vityātaramaṅkaiyiṉ tīrāppaciyaippōkki,
       appāttirattil mēṉmēlum amutu vaḷarappeṟṟu, annakariluḷḷa
       ulakavaṟiyeṉṉum ampalattaiyaṭaintu, aṅkē vanta ellāvuyirkaḷiṉ
       paciyaiyum pōkki, avvaṟañ ceytalaiyē nityaniyamamākap
       pūṇṭiruntaṉaḷ. aṅṅaṉamirukkumpōtu, cōḻaṉ makaṉāṉa utayakumaraṉ
       taṉṉai virumpi maṟumuṟai avviṭam vara, ataṉaiyaṟinta maṇimēkalai
       avaṉ taṉṉai aṟintukoḷḷāvaṇṇam vityātara maṅkaiyākiya
       kāyacaṇṭikaiyiṉ vaṭivaṅkoṇṭu annakarattuc ciṟaiccālaiyai yaṭaintu
       āṅkup pacittiruppavark kellām uṇavaḷittu ataṉai aṟaccālai yākkiṉāḷ.
       Page 46
       appōtu avviṭattun taṉṉai viṭātutoṭarnta utayakumaraṉ,
       kāyacaṇṭikaiyiṉ kaṇavaṉākiya vityātaraṉāl taṉ maṉaiviyai virumpi
       vantavaṉeṉṟukaruti, vāḷāl veṭṭappaṭṭu vīḻntatukaṇṭu maṉaṅkalaṅkip
       piṉpu kantiṟpāvaiyeṉṉun teyvantēṟṟat tēṟi, utayakumaraṉ
       ṟantaiyākiya cōḻaṉāl cantēkattiṉmēṟ ciṟai vaikkappaṭṭu avaṉmaṉaivi
       irācamātēviyiṉ muyaṟciyālē acciṟaiyiṉiṉṟum viṭupaṭṭaṉaḷ. piṉ
       maṇimēkalai, kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattiṉiṉṟu cāvakanāṭṭuḷḷa nākapurattai
       yaṭaintu, ataṉaracaṉāṉa puṇṇiyarācaṉōṭu maṇipallavañ cārntu,
       aṅkuḷḷa puttapīṭikaiyai avaṉukkuk kāṭṭi ataṉāl
       avvaracaṉatu paḻampiṟappai avaṉukkaṟivittāḷ. akkālattuk
       kāvirippaṭṭiṉam kaṭalkoḷḷappaṭṭateṉṟa ceytiyaiyum, taṉ tāyāṉa
       mātaviyum aṟavaṇavaṭikaḷum vañcimānakariliruttalaiyum
       tīvatilakaiyāl aṟintu, av
       vañcinōkki eḻuntavaḷ, mutalil, ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉā leṭuppikkappaṭṭa
       kaṇṇakikōṭṭamaṭaintu tāyākiya ap pattiṉikkaṭavuḷait taricittut
       taṉṉetirkālacceytikaḷai akkaṭavuḷālaṟiyappeṟṟapiṉ, ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉāḷum
       an nakaraiyaṭaintu, ataṉuḷḷē piravēcittāḷ; piravēcittavaḷ,
       ataṉ vaḷaṅkaḷaiyellāṅ kaṇṭu makiḻntu, āṅku vacitta camayavātikaḷ
       palarōṭum aḷavaḷāvi avvaccamayat tiṟaṅkaḷai aṟintukoṇṭaṉaḷ.
       ataṉpiṉpu, annakarattuḷḷa pautta vikārattil tavañ ceytukoṇṭirunta
       kōvalaṉ tantai mācāttuvāṉaikkaṇṭu paṇiya, avaṉ, avaḷ tāyum
       aṟavaṇavaṭikaḷum kāñcimānakarañ ceṉṟirut talaiyum avvaṭikaḷ avaṭkut
       tarumōpatēcañceyya eṇṇiyiruttalaiyuṅ kūṟiyatōṭu, annāṭṭil
       maḻaiyiṉmaiyāl uyirkaḷ vāṭutaliṉ, amutacurapiyuṭaṉ avaḷ aṅkuc
       cellavēṇṭiya āvaciyakattaiyum maṇimēkalaikku vaṟpuṟuttiṉaṉ.
       Page 47
       ivaṟṟait taṉ pāṭṭaṉvāyk kēṭṭatum avaḷ vañciyiṉiṉṟum
       uṭaṉēpuṟappaṭṭuk kāñci mānakaramaṭaintu, annakariṟ paciyāl
       varuntivāṭiya uyirkaṭkellāmuṇavaḷittut taṉtāyuṭaṉ
       aṟavaṇavaṭikaḷaiyum āṅkuk kaṇṭu vaṇaṅki, avvaṭikaḷāl pauttamata
       tarumaṅkaḷaṉaittum upatēcikkappeṟṟup piṉ muttipeṟutaṟporuṭṭu
       akkāñciyilēyē tavañceytu koṇṭiruntaṉaḷ.
       mēṟkāṭṭiya iraṇṭu carittiraṅkaḷum ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ campantam peṟṟu avaṉ
       varalāṟukaḷ palavaṟṟai aṟivikkak kūṭiyaṉavāyirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivviraṇṭu
       carittiranūlkaḷuḷ, cilappatikārattiṉ iṟutippakutiyāṉa vañcikkāṇṭa
       muḻutum, ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ, kaṇṇakiyiṉ paṭivañ camaikkavēṇṭi
       imayamalaiyiṟ kalleṭuttuk kaṅkaiyil nīrāṭṭuvataṟkum, ammuyaṟciyil
       taṉṉaiyikaḻnta āriyavaracarai veṟṟikoḷvataṟkumāka nikaḻttiya
       vaṭanāṭṭu yāttiraiyaiyum, ataṉpiṉ nikaḻnta accēraṉ ceytikaḷaiyum
       mikutiyākak kūṟuvatu. ātaliṉ, akkāṇṭattē iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ
       kūṟiyiruppavai perumpālum ivvārāyccikkup peritum
       upakāramāyiruttalāl, avvaṭikaḷatu ceyyuṇaṭaiyai ēṟṟapeṟṟi
       vacaṉanaṭaippaṭuttukiṉṟēm: ikkāṇṭamāṉatu (1) kuṉṟakkuravai, (2)
       kāṭcikkātai, (3) kālkōṭkātai, (4) nīrppaṭaikkātai,(5)
       naṭukaṟkātai,(6) vāḻttukkātai, (7) varantarukātai eṉa
       iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷāl ēḻu pirivukaḷākkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.

       page 48
       5-m atikāram:-
       ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉatu vaṭa nāṭṭiyāttirai.
       1. kuṉṟakkuravai.
       vēṭuvar taṅkaḷ kuṟicciyil kaṇṇaki poruṭṭuk kuravaik kūttāṭiyatu.
       ~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~
       kaṇṇaki taṉ kaṇavaṉaiyiḻanta peruntuyarōṭum vaiyaikkarai vaḻiyē
       mēṟṟicainōkkic ceṉṟu, ceṅkuṉṟu eṉṉum malaiyaṭaintu āṅku oru vēṅkai
       marattiṉ kīḻēniṟpa, avviṭattirunta malaivēṭuvariṟcilar
       akkaṇṇakiyaineruṅki avaḷainōkki, 'malaivēṅkai naṟuniḻaliṉ
       vaḷḷipōlvīr maṉanaṭuṅka - mulaiyiḻantu vantu niṉṟīr yāvirō?' eṉṟu
       kēṭpa, ataṟkavaḷ ciṟituṅ kōpiyāmalē, 'maṇamaturaiyō ṭaracukēṭuṟa
       valviṉaivan turutta kālaik - kaṇavaṉaiyaṅ kiḻantupōnta kaṭuviṉaiyēṉ
       yāṉeṉṟāḷ.' iṅṅaṉam kaṇṇakikūṟa, malaivāṇar kēṭṭu añci avaḷai
       vaṇaṅkiniṉṟapōtu, tēvarkuḻām āṅku vantu malarmaḻai poḻintu
       aṅkuḷḷavar kaṇmuṉpē
       kaṇṇakikku avaḷ kaṇavaṉ kōvalaṉaikkāṭṭi, avaḷai yumuṭaṉaḻaittuk
       koṇṭu viṇṇulakañ celvārāyiṉar. ivvaṟputa nikaḻcciyai nēriṟkaṇṭu
       kaḷitta avvēṭuvar, iṅku vantu niṉṟa mātarāḷ nam kulattukkē oru
       perun teyvamāvaḷ; ivaḷporuṭṭu nām kuravaiyāṭikkoṇṭāṭuvōm' eṉṟu,
       tammavarayellām oruṅkaḻaittu -
       Page 49
       "ciṟukuṭiyīrē! ciṟukuṭi yīrē!
       teyvaṅ koḷḷumiṉ ciṟukuṭi yīrē!
       niṟaṅkiḷa raruvip paṟampiṉ tāḻvarai
       naṟuñciṉai vēṅkai naṉṉiḻaṟ kīḻōr
       teyvaṅ koḷḷumiṉ ciṟukuṭi yīrē!
       toṇṭakan toṭumiṉ ciṟupaṟai toṭumiṉ
       kōṭuvāy vaimmiṉ koṭumaṇi yiyakkumuiṉ
       kuṟiñci pāṭumiṉ naṟumpukai yeṭumiṉ
       pūppali ceymmiṉ kāppukkaṭai niṟumiṉ
       paravalum paravumiṉ viravumalar tūvumiṉ
       orumulai yiḻanta naṅkaikkup
       perumalai tuñcātu vaḷañcurak keṉavē"
       eṉṟu ārpparitteḻuntu, malaināṭṭu muṟaippaṭi, kuravaikkūttāṭiyum,
       ataṟkuriya pāṭalkaḷaippāṭiyum peruñ ciṟappuc ceytārkaḷ. iṅku
       iṅṅaṉam nikaḻntatu.

       2. kāṭcikkātai.
       kaṇṇakiyiṉ varalāṟaṟinta ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ, pattiṉikkup paṭivañ
       camaittaṟku imayattiṉiṉṟu kalleṭuttuvara eṇṇiyatu.
       - ------------------------------
       imayavarampaṉ - neṭuñcēralātaṉ tōṉṟalākiya ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ vañciyiluḷḷa
       ilavantikai veḷḷimāṭam eṉṟa māḷikaiyil taṉtēvi iḷaṅkōvēṇmāḷuṭaṉ*
       vacittu vanta kālaiyil, mañcucūḻuñ
       cōlaikaḷaiyuṭaiya malaivaḷattaik kaṇṭukaḷikka eṇṇiyavaṉāy –

       * 'iḷaṅkō vēṇmā ḷuṭaṉirun taruḷi' eṉavarum mūlattukku

       eṉṟumām." eṉṟeḻutiṉār. itaṉāl, tampi iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷuṭaṉum, maṉaivi
       vēṇmāḷuṭaṉum ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉiruntāṉ eṉṟu attoṭarkku uraikūṟuvatum
       avvuraiyāciriyar karuttātal viḷaṅkum. iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ, tam
       tamayaṉuṭaṉ taṅkiyiruntava reṉpatu, patikattālum teritalāl

       Page 50

       tēṉeṭukkuṅ kuṟavar nikaḻttum
       ōcaiyum, paṟaiyōcaipōṉṟa aruviyiṉ oliyum, puliyōṭu porukiṉṟa
       yāṉaiyiṉ muḻakkamum, tiṉaippuṉaṅ kāvaliṟ paraṇmicaiyiruppōruṭaiya
       kūppīṭukaḷum, yāṉai piṭikkum iṭaṅkaḷilē kuḻiyilvīḻnta vēḻaṅkaḷaip
       pākar
       paḻakkumu captamum, āṅkucceṉṟa cēṉaikaḷatu āravārattōṭu
       kalantolittaṉa. annilaiyil, malaivāṇarākiya vēṭuvar palar
       oruṅkukūṭi,
       "yāṉaiveṇ kōṭu makiliṉ kuppaiyum
       māṉmayirk kavariyum* matuviṉ kuṭaṅkaḷum
       cantaṉak kuṟaiyum cinturak kaṭṭiyum
       añcaṉat tiraḷum aṇiyari tāramum
       ēla valliyum iruṅkaṟi valliyum
       kūvai nīṟuṅ koḻuṅkoṭik kavalaiyum
       teṅkiṉ paḻaṉun tēmāṅ kaṉiyum
       paiṅkoṭip paṭalaiyum palaviṉ paḻaṅkaḷum
       kāyamuṅ karumpum pūmali koṭiyum

       peruṅkulai vāḻaiyi ṉiruṅkaṉit tāṟum
       āḷiyi ṉaṇaṅkum ariyiṉ kuruḷaiyum

       *māṉmayirākiya veṇcāmarai.

       tiṟaikaḷuṭaṉ vāyiliṟ kāttuniṟkum pakaiyaracarpōla, am malaivāṇar
       ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ tirumuṉpu vantu niṉṟu 'ēḻpiṟappaṭiyēm, vāḻkaniṉ koṟṟam'
       eṉṟu avaṉaṭi paṇintu," vēntar vēntē! yām vāḻum malaiyiṉkaṇuḷḷa

       uṭaṉaḻaittukkoṇṭu eṅkaḷ kaṇmuṉpē viṇṇulakañ ceṉṟaṉar;
       Page 52
       (icceyti cilappatikārap patikattuk kaṇṭatu.)avaḷ ennāṭṭāḷ kollō!
       yāvar makaḷō, aṟiyēm. itu periyatōr aticayantarā niṉṟatu; tēvarīr
       nāṭṭil nikaḻnta itaṉai aṟintaruḷal vēṇṭum" eṉṟu tāṅkaḷ nēriṟkaṇṭa
       ceytikaḷai aracaṉukkukkūṟi avaṉai vāḻttiniṉṟaṉar.
       appōtu aracaṉatu peruñciṟappiṉaiyum, malaivaḷattiṉ māṭciyaiyuṅ
       kaṇṭu kaḷippuṟṟirunta maturaiccāttaṉār, (kōvalaṉ kolaiyuṇṭatu
       mutalāya ceytikaḷ tammūril nikaḻntavaṟṟai nēril aṟintavarātalāl)
       ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉai nōkki "vēntē! yāṉaṟivēṉ, atu nikaḻntavāṟṟai" eṉṟu
       toṭaṅki, taṉtēviyiṉ cilampu tiruṭiyavaṉeṉṟu kaṇṇaki kaṇavaṉaip
       pāṇṭiyaṉ kolaipurivittataiyum, aḵtaṟinta kaṇṇaki peruñciṉaṅkoṇṭu
       pāṇṭiyaṉmuṉ ceṉṟu vaḻakkāṭi veṉṟatum, pāṇṭiyaṉtēvi muṉpu maṟṟoru
       cilampai vīciyeṟintuviṭṭuk kaṇṇaki vañciṉaṅkūṟit taṉ orumulaiyait
       tirukivīci, atiṉiṉṟeḻunta tīyāl maturai mūtūraic cuṭṭerittatum,
       ciṅkātaṉattiruntu kaṇṇakiyiṉ vaḻakkaikkēṭṭa neṭuñceḻiyaṉ tāṉceyta
       koṭuṅkōṉmaikku āṟṟātu taṉ ācaṉattē vīḻntiṟantatum, iṅṅaṉam pāṇṭiyaṉ
       iṟakkavum, avaṉ kōpperuntēviyum kalakkaṅkoḷḷātē uṭaṉuyir viṭṭatum
       viḷaṅkakkūṟiviṭṭup piṉ, 'pāṇṭiyaṉatu koṭuṅkōṉmai ittaṉmaittu
       eṉpataip peruvēntaṉākiya uṉṉiṭattuk kūṟavantavaḷpōlat taṉakkuriya
       cōṇāṭu cellātu niṉṉāṭu aṭaivāḷāyiṉaḷ annaṅkai' eṉṟu accāttaṉār
       colli muṭittaṉar.
       Page 53
       iṅṅaṉam pāṇṭiyaṉukku nērnta tīviṉaittiṟaṅkaḷai yellāṅkēṭṭa
       ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ, varuttamikkavaṉāyc cāttaṉārai nōkki " pulavīr! tāṉ
       ceṅkōliṉiṉṟu vaḻuviya ceyti emmaiyotta aracar cevikaḷiṟ
       ceṉṟeṭṭuvataṟku muṉpē pāṇṭiyaṉ uyir nīṅkiyatāṉatu, avaṉatu
       tīviṉaiyāl vaḷaikkappaṭṭa kōlai uṭaṉē nimirac ceytu
       ceṅkōlākkiviṭṭatu. aracarāyuḷḷārkku, nāṭṭil maḻai kālattiṟ
       peyyātāyiṉ accam; uyirkaḷ tavaṟu ceyyumāyiṉ accam; koṭuṅkōṟkañci
       maṉṉuyirkaḷaik kāttaṟkuriya uyarkuṭiyiṟpiṟattaliṟ ṟuṉpamallatu
       cukamēyillai" eṉṟu kūṟiviṭṭut taṉ tēviyai nōkki " naṉṉutāl!
       kaṇavaṉākiya pāṇṭiyaṉuṭaṉ taṉṉuyirai nītta avaṉ tēviyum, ciṉattuṭaṉ
       nam nāṭunōkkivanta kaṇṇakiyumākiya ivviruperumpattiṉikaḷuḷḷē yāvar
       viyakkat takkavarāvar?" eṉṟu ucāva, ataṟku vēṇmāḷ ' taṉ kaṇavaṉatu
       tuṉpattaik kāṇātu uyirnītta pāṇṭiyaṉ peruntēvi viṇṇulakattē
       peruntiruvuṟakkaṭavaḷ.atuniṟka; nam nāṭṭai nōkkivanta pattiṉiyai
       nām vaḻipaṭutal iṉṟiyamaiyātatu' eṉṟu kūṟiṉāḷ. atu kēṭṭa aracaṉ,
       avaḷ kūṟiyatai virumpiyēṟṟut taṉ amaiccarai nōkkavum, avarkaḷ, 'nam
       nāṭu nōkkivanta appattiṉikkaṭavuṭkup paṭivañcamaittaṟkuriya
       cilaiyaip potiyamalaiyiṉiṉṟēṉum imayamalaiyiṉiṉṟēṉum eṭuttuvarutalē
       takkatām. āyiṉ, potiyattiṉiṉṟu koṇarvataik kāviriyiṉum
       imayattiṉiṉṟu koṇarvataik kaṅkaiyiṉum nīrāṭṭit tūymaiceyvittal
       mikap porutta muṭaiyatākum' eṉṟu kūṟiṉar.
       Page 54
       kūṟakkēṭṭa ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ 'potiyamalaiyiṟ kalleṭuttuk kāvirittuṟaiyil
       nīrāṭṭutal peruvīrarākiya cēravamicattavarkkuc ciṟappait taruñ
       ceykaiyaṉṟu; ātalāl, imayattiliruntu kalleṭuppittuk kaṅkaiyil
       nīrāṭṭivarutalē nam perumaik kēṟṟatām. imavāṉ nam viruppattiṟ
       kiṇaṅkāṉāyiṉ(appakkattār naṅkaruttuk kiṇaṅkātu taṭuttu niṟparāyiṉ
       eṉpatu karuttu.) iṅkuniṉṟum vañcimālai cūṭic cēṉaikaḷuṭaṉ ceṉṟu
       puṟattuṟaikkamainta nam vīracceyalkaḷ palavaṟṟaiyum āṅkukkāṇpippēṉ'
       eṉṟu vīrāvēcattuṭaṉ kūṟiṉāṉ.
       iṅṅaṉam ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ kūṟiya vīramoḻikaḷaik kēṭṭa villavaṉ kōtai eṉṟa
       cēṉāpati aracaṉai vāḻttikkoṇṭu kūṟuvāṉ:- "vēntar vēntē! nummaiyotta
       vēntarāṉa cōḻapāṇṭiyar nummōṭu pakaittuk koṅkar pōrkkaḷattē tam
       pulik koṭiyaiyum mīṉakkoṭiyaiyum yuttakaḷattiliḻantu ōṭiṉarāyiṉum,
       ac ceyti tikkayaṅkaḷiṉ cevivarai ceṉṟu paravalāyiṟṟu. koṅkaṇar,
       kaliṅkar, karunaṭar, paṅkaḷar, kaṅkar, kaṭṭiyar vaṭavāriyar
       ivaruṭaṉ tamatu tamiḻppaṭai kalantuporuta cerukkaḷattil tām
       yāṉaiyaiviṭṭup pakaivarai yaḻitta ariyaceykai iṉṉum eṅkaḷ
       kaṇkaḷaiviṭṭu nīṅkavillai.aṉṟiyum emkōmakaḷāy viḷaṅkiya
       ("eṅkōmakaḷeṉṟatu ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉmātāvai; ivaḷai ivaṉ koṇṭupōyt
       tīrttamāṭṭiṉatoru katai" eṉpar, arumpatavuraiyāciriyar.
       ivvākkiyattiṉiṉṟu, ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ taṉ tāyiṉ cilaiyaiyaṉṟi avaḷaiyē
       uṭaṉaḻaittuc ceṉṟu nīrāṭṭuvittāṉ eṉpatum avvuraiyāciriyar
       karuttākat terikiṉṟatu.)
       Page 55
       tam tāyiṉcilaiyaik kaṅkaiyiṟ ṟīrttamāṭṭiyakālattē etirttuvanta
       āriya varacar āyiravarmuṉ tām oruvarākaniṉṟu poruta pōrk kōlattaik
       kaṭuṅkaṭkūṟṟamum, kaṇviḻittu nōkkiya taṉṟō. iṅṅaṉam, nīrcūḻnta in
       nilavulakattai veṉṟu tamiḻnāṭākacceyta tām vaṭanāṭṭil
       yāttiraiceyyak karutiṉ, āṅku nummai etirppavar yāvarō?
       imayamalaikku eṅkōṉākiya nīvir cellakkarutiyiruppatu pattiṉik
       kaṭavuḷaic camaittaṟkuriya cilaiyiṉporuṭṭē yātalāl, āṅkuvāḻum
       aracarkkellām, vilkayal puliyivaṟṟai ilacciṉaiyākak koṇṭa num
       tirumukattai muṉṉē viṭuttaruḷal vēṇṭum' eṉṟu kūṟiṉaṉ. itukēṭṭa
       aḻumpilvēḷ eṉṉum amaiccaṉ "in nāvalantīvil nam pakaivarāyuḷḷāratu
       oṟṟarkaḷ iv vañcimānakaraiviṭṭu nīṅkupavarallar. iv voṟṟukkaḷē
       pakaiyaracar cevikaḷil nam vaṭanāṭṭiyāttiraipaṟṟiya ceytikaḷai
       aṟivikkat takkaṉa. ataṉāl nam yāttiraiyaippaṟṟi, ivvūriṟ
       paṟaiyaṟaintu terivittaloṉṟē pōtiyatu" eṉṟu uraikkac ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉum
       ataṟku uṭampaṭṭaṉaṉ. piṉṉarp pēriyāṟṟiṉiṉṟucu puṟappaṭṭu aracaṉ taṉ
       perum parivāraṅkaḷuṭaṉ vañcimā- nakaraṭaintāṉ. aṭaintatum, yāṉaimēl
       muracēṟṟappeṟṟuc ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉatu vaṭa nāṭṭiyāttiraipaṟṟiyum,
       pakaiyaracar vantu paṇiyāviṭil
       avarkku nēruṅ kēṭukaḷaippaṟṟiyum,
       "vāḻka eṅkō maṉṉavar peruntakai
       ūḻitō ṟūḻi yulakaṅ kākkeṉa
       viṟṟalaik koṇṭa viyaṉpē rimayattōr
       kaṟkoṇṭu peyarumeṅ kāvala ṉātaliṉ
       Page 56
       vaṭaticai maruṅkiṉ maṉṉa rellām
       iṭutiṟai koṭuvan tetirī rāyiṉ
       kaṭaṟkaṭam peṟinta kaṭumpōr vārttaiyum
       viṭarccilai poṟitta viyaṉperu vārttaiyuṅ*
       kēṭṭu vāḻumiṉ kēḷī rāyiṉ
       tōṭṭuṇai† tuṟakkun tuṟavoṭu vāḻumiṉ
       tāḻkaḻaṉ maṉṉaṉ taṉṟiru mēṉi‡
       vāḻka cēṉā mukam."
       eṉṟu ūreṅkum paṟai aṟaiyappaṭṭatu.
       ---------
       * 'etirēṟṟuk kāṇīrāyiṉ, kaṭaluṭpukkum malaiyiṉēṟiyum vāḻumiṉ eṉpār,
       iraṇṭuvārttaiyuṅ kēṭṭu vāḻumiṉ – eṉṟār' eṉpatu arumpatavurai.
       † tōṭṭuṇai-maṉaiviyar
       ‡ 'tirumēṉiyākiya cēṉāmukam; cēṉāmukam aracaṉukkuc ciṟantamaiyiṉ,
       tirumēṉi eṉṟār' eṉpatu arumpatavurai.

       3. kālkōṭ kātai.
       ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ vaṭanāṭṭiṟceyta perumpōrum, imayañceṉṟu
       pattiṉikkuk kalleṭuppittatum.
       ~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~
       iṅṅaṉam vañcimānakariṟ paṟaiyaṟaiyappaṭṭapiṉṉar, cēraṉ -
       ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ aṉṟu mālaiyil ācāṉ peruṅkaṇi amaiccarkaḷum
       tāṉaittalaivarun taṉṉai vāḻtti niṟkac ciṅkātaṉattē vīṟṟiruntu taṉ
       cēṉātipatikaḷai nōkki aṭiyil varumāṟu kūṟuvāṉāyiṉāṉ:- "āriyamaṉṉar
       palarum tamnāṭṭilnikaḻnta cuyamvaramoṉṟaṉ poruṭṭuk kuḻumiyirunta
       peruṅkūṭṭattē - 'teṉṟamiḻ nāṭāḷum vēntar ceruvēṭṭup pukaṉṟeḻuntu -
       miṉṟavaḻu mimaya neṟṟiyil viḷaṅkuviṟpuli kayalpoṟitta nāḷ –
       Page 57
       empōlum muṭimaṉṉar īṅkillaippōlum' eṉṟu nakaittikaḻneṉar eṉat,
       tīrtta yāttirai ceytu koṇṭu iṅkuvanta imayattāpatarāl aṟintēm. (ic
       ceyti, vāḻttuk kātaiyiṉ uraippāṭṭu maṭaiyiṟ kaṇṭatu.) av viḻimoḻi
       nampālē taṅkumāyiṉ, aḵtu emakkumaṭṭu maṉṟi empōṉṟa

       ātaliṉ, aṅṅaṉam ikaḻcciceyta vaṭaticai maṉṉaratu muṭittalaiyil
       pattiṉikkaṭavuḷaic camaittaṟkuriya kallai ēṟṟikkoṇṭu varuvēṉ.
       aṅṅaṉañ ceyyātu eṉ kaivāḷ vaṟitēvarumāyiṉ, eṉṉoṭu muraṇiya
       pakaiyaracarai naṭuṅkacceytaṭakkāmal, vaḷantaṅkiya eṉṉāṭṭuk
       kuṭikaḷai varuttuṅ koṭuṅkōlaṉākak kaṭavēṉ" - eṉṟu appērattāṇiyiṟ
       ciṉattuṭaṉ vañciṉaṅkūṟiṉaṉ. iṅṅaṉam aracaṉ kūṟiyataik kēṭṭa ācāṉ

       ilaṅkaiyil naṭattiya pōraiyum, tērūrntu nikaḻttiya avaṉatu pāratac
       ceruvaiyum itaṉōṭu orucēravaittup pāṭiyatuṭaṉ, ippōrkkaḷattē
       pakaivaratu muṭittalaikaḷaiyē aṭuppākavum, piṭarikaḷaiyē
       tāḻiyākavum, valayamaṇinta tōḷkaḷaiyē tuṭuppākavum koṇṭu camaitta
       ūṉcōṟṟaip pēy
       maṭaiyaṉ avvappēyiṉ takutiyaṟintu parimāṟa vayiṟāra uṇṭu kaḷittut,
       tarumayuttattāl tamakkittakaiya peruviruntuceyta ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉai
       'ūḻiyaḷavu vāḻka' eṉṟu vāyāra vāḻttuvaṉavum āyiṉa.
       ---
       * neṟkaḷattiṟ kaṭāviṭṭuḻakkutal.
       iṅṅaṉamāka, āriyappaṭaiyaiveṉṟu tāṉiṉaitta kāriyaṅkaḷuḷoṉṟai
       muṭittukkoṇṭa ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ taṉ tāṉaittalaivaṉākiya villavaṉkōtaiyai
       nōkki, 'vaṭaticaiyuḷ nāṉmaṟaiyāḷum nityākkiṉi vaḷarttalaiyē
       peruvāḻvāka uṭaiyavarumākiya antaṇapperiyōraic cellumiṭaṅkaḷilellām
       pōṟṟik kākkak kaṭavīr' eṉṟu kūṟip perumpaṭaiyuṭaṉ avaṉaiyēvi,
       pattiṉik kaṭavuḷaip poṟittaṟkuriya cilaiyai imayamalaiyiṉiṉṟu
       koṇṭuvarumpaṭi ceytu akkāriyattaiyum muṭittuk koṇṭaṉaṉ.

       4.nīrppaṭaikkātai.
       ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ pattiṉippaṭivattaik kaṅkai nīrāṭṭiyatum,
       taṉṉāṭu tirumpiyatum.
       -----------------------
       mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu, tamiḻaratu āṟṟalaiyaṟiyātu taṉṉuṭaṉ malainta āriyac
       cēṉaikaḷaik kūṟṟuvaṉukkut toḻil perukumpaṭi koṉṟu, tēvācurayuttam
       patiṉeṭṭāṇṭilum, irāmarāvaṇayuttam patiṉeṭṭu mātattum, pāratayuttam
       patiṉeṭṭu nāḷilum, ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉum kaṉavicayarum purintapōr patiṉeṭṭu
       nāḻikaiyilum muṭintaṉaveṉṟu, ulakōr ippōraiyum kūṭṭiyeṇṇik
       koḷḷumpaṭi orupakaṟkuḷḷē pakaivarai veṉṟuviḷaṅkiya
       cēraṉ-ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ, ciṉamikka taṉ cēṉaikaḷāl pattiṉik kaṭavuṭkuriya
       cilaiyai imayattiṉiṉṟu meṭuppitta piṉṉar, taṉṉuṭaṉ
       pōrkkōlaṅkoṇṭeḻunta kaṉaka vicayaratu katirmuṭimēl
       accilaiyaiyēṟṟikkoṇṭu ataṉai nīrāṭṭit tūymai ceytaṟporuṭṭuk
       kaṅkaippēriyāṟṟiṉ vaṭakaraivantu cērntāṉ. avaṉ aṅku vantatum,
       pattiṉiyiṉcilai ākamamvallōrāl kaṅkānatiyil muṟaippaṭi
       nīrāṭṭappaṭṭatu. itu muṭintapiṉṉarc cēṉaiyuṭaṉum parivāraṅkaḷuṭaṉum
       cērar perumāṉ nāvāykaḷ mūlam ap pēriyāṟṟaik kaṭantu ataṉ
       teṉkaraiyiṟ piravēcittaṉaṉ. ammānatiyiṉ teṉkarai veḷipparappilē,
       āriya maṉṉarum naṭpāḷarumākiya kaṉṉarāl jayacīlaṉākavaruñ cērar
       peruntakai paṭaikaḷuṭaṉ vantu taṅkutaṟkeṉṟu, aracarkkuriya kōyilum
       aḻakiya maṇṭapaṅkaḷum irāca capaikaḷum
       Page 67
       pūmpantarum urimaip paḷḷiyum tāmaraip poykaiyum āṭaraṅkukaḷum
       maṟṟum peruvēntarkku vēṇṭiyaṉavellām mikavum aḻakupeṟa
       amaikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ teṉkarai pukkatum
       avvaḻakiyapāṭiyil taṉ parivāraṅkaḷōṭuñ ceṉṟu taṅkiṉāṉ.
       iṅṅaṉam pāṭiyiṟṟaṅkiyapiṉ, cērarperumāṉ, etirtta māṟṟaracaratu
       maṉavūkkaṅ keṭumpaṭi pōril tam vīrac ceyalkaḷaik kāṭṭi
       vīracuvarkkamaṭainta tāṉait talaivarkaḷum, appōrilē aṭarntuḻakkit
       talaiyun tōḷum vilaipeṟa aṟupaṭṭukkiṭantavarkaḷum, vāḷāṟceyyum
       viṉaikaḷellāñceytu pakaivarai aḻittumuṭintavarkaḷum, uṟaviṉarōṭu
       tammakaḷirum uṭaṉmaṭiyumpaṭi vīḻntavarkaḷumākiya iṟantupaṭṭa
       vīrarkaḷuṭaiya maintaraiyum; tūcip paṭaiyil niṉṟu māṟṟāraiveṉṟu
       vākaimālai cūṭiyavarkaḷum, pakaivaratu tērvīraraikkoṉṟu
       avarkaḷuṭaiya kurutiyōṭu polintuniṉṟavarkaḷum, pakaivaratu
       karuntalaikaḷaik kūṟṟuvaṉuṅ kaṇṭiraṅkumpaṭi orucēra aṟuttu
       veṟṟipeṟṟavarkaḷum, kavacañcitaiya mārpil viḻuppuṇpaṭṭu māṟṟāraip
       puṟaṅkaṇṭu mīṇṭavarkaḷum ākiya (iṟavāta) ellāvīrarkaḷaiyum
       taṉṉiṭattu varumpaṭiyaḻaittu,avaraṭainta veṟṟikku aṟikuṟiyāka
       avarkaṭkellām poṉṉālākiya vākaippūkkaḷai, tāṉ piṟantanāḷiṟ ceyyum
       peruṅkoṭaiyiṉum mikutiyāka neṭumpōtiruntu koṭuttu vekumāṉittāṉ.
       iṅṅaṉam paṉampūvait tumpai mālaiyuṭaṉ cūṭip pulavarpāṭutaṟkuriya
       puṟattuṟaikaḷellām muṭittu jayacīlaṉākac cēraṉ - ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ
       taṉṉaracirukkaiyil vīṟṟirukkumpōtu,
       Page 68
       māṭalaṉ eṉṉum maṟaiyavaṉ āṅku etirpārātē vantuniṉṟu aracaṉai
       ācikūṟivāḻtti viṭṭu, ' eṅkōvē! mātaviyeṉṉum nāṭakakkaṇikaiyiṉ
       kaṭaṟkaraippāṭalāṉatu, kaṉakavicayaratu muṭittalaikaḷai iṅṅaṉam
       nerittuviṭṭatu; itu viyakkattakkatām' eṉṟu oru vārttaiyaik kūṟiṉāṉ.
       itaṉaikkēṭṭa ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ avaṉ karuttai aṟiyātavaṉāy,
       'nāṉmaṟaiyāḷaṉē! pakaippulattaracarum aṟintirāta nakaiccolloṉṟai
       iṅkuvantu tiṭīreṉak kūṟiṉai; nī coṉṉa uraipporuḷ yātu?
       viḷaṅkaccolluka' eṉṟāṉ; eṉalum ammaṟaiyavaṉ collaluṟṟāṉ.
       "kuṭavarkōvē! kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattil nikaḻnta kaṭalviḷaiyāṭṭiṟkut
       taṉ kātalaṉākiya kōvalaṉuṭaṉ ceṉṟirunta mātaviyeṉṉum nāṭakakkaṇikai
       karuttuvēṟupaṭap pāṭiya kāṉalvarippāṭṭuṭaṉ ūḻviṉaiyum uṭaṉ
       cērnturuttamaiyāl kōvalaṉ akkaṇikaiyai veṟuttuppirintu, taṉmaṉaivi
       kaṇṇakiyai uṭaṉaḻaittukkoṇṭu maturaimānakaram pukuntāṉ; pukuntavaṉ,
       annakarattaracaṉāṉa pāṇṭiyaṉ uyirnīttu viṇṇulakaṭaiyumpaṭi
       kolaiyuṇṭaṉa ṉaṉṟō. aṅṅaṉaṅ kolaiyuṇṭa kōvalaṉ
       kaṟpuṭaimaṉaiviyāṉavaḷ uṉ nāṭṭai aṭaintamaiyālaṉṟō, appattiṉi
       ippōtu vaṭaticaiyacaruṭaiya maṇimuṭiyilēṟi viḷaṅkuvāḷāyiṉaḷ"

       kāraṇattaiyum kēṭpāyāka;
       page 69
       akattiya muṉivaruṭaiya potiyamalaiyai valañceytukoṇṭu
       kaṉṉiyākumarittuṟaiyiṟ ṟīrttamāṭi mīṇṭuvarukiṉṟa yāṉ,eṉ ūḻviṉaip
       payaṉpōlum, pāṇṭiyaṉatu maturaimānakaram ceṉṟēṉ. aṅkē nāṉ
       taṅkiyapōtu, pāṇṭiyaṉ taṉkaṇavaṉai aniyāyamākak kolvitta
       koṭuṅkōṉmaiyait taṉ cilampaikkoṇṭu av varacaṉukku viḷakki
       vaḻakkāṭiveṉṟāḷ kaṇṇaki eṉṟaceyti ūrmuḻutum paraviyatu.
       ikkolaicceyti kēṭṭa āycciyartalaiviyākiya mātari (kōvalaṉkaṇṇakiyai
       aṭaikkalamākappeṟṟavaḷ) iṭaitteruviluḷḷa tāterumaṉṟattiṉiṉṟum
       eḻuntuceṉṟu, 'iṭaikkulamakkāḷ! antō, aṭaikkalapporuḷai
       iḻantukeṭṭēṉ; kōvalaṉ kuṟṟamuṭaiyavaṉallaṉ; aracaṉē tavaṟiṉāṉ;
       ceṅkōlum veṇkuṭaiyum iṅṅaṉam piḻaipaṭuṅkālamum nērntaṉavō!'eṉṟalaṟi
       iraviṉ naṭuccāmattē eriyiṟpukuntu māyntaṉaḷ. kōvalaṉ kaṇṇaki
       iruvaraiyum tammuṭaṉaḻaittukkoṇṭu maturaivanta kavuntiyaṭikaḷ,
       kōvalaṉai aracaṉ kolai ceytatukēṭṭatum uṇṭākiya peruñcīṟṟamāṉatu
       avvaracaṉatu maraṇattāl māṟiyatāyiṉum, 'eṉ aṉpukkuriya ivarkaṭku
       ivviṉaiyum varakkaṭavatō' eṉṟiraṅki uṇṇānōṉpukoṇṭu
       uyarkatiyaṭaintaṉaḷ. aṉṟiyum, kaṇṇakiyiṉ cīṟṟattāl maturaimānakaram
       erikkappaṭṭatu. ivaṟṟaiyellām maturaiyil nēriluṇarnta yāṉ, piṉpu
       eṉṉūrākiya kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉam ceṉṟu, eṉṉaṇpaṉāṉa (kōvalaṉukku ivaṉ
       naṇpaṉeṉpatum, maturaiyil avaṉuṭaṉ ivvantaṇaṉ aḷavaḷāviyatum, innūl
       38m pakkattuk kāṇka.) kōvalaṉ kolaimutaliyavaṟṟāl yāṉaṭainta
       tuyaraṅkaḷai āṅkuḷḷa periyōrkaṭku uraikkalāyiṉēṉ.

       avvāṟuraitta ceyti ciṟituciṟitāka ūreṅkumparavi muṭivil

       taṉmaintaṉukkum marukikkum pāṇṭiyaṉukkum nērnta koṭun tuṉpaṅkaḷaic

       uttamatāṉaṅkaḷiṟ celavu ceytuviṭṭup, pautta caṅkattār irukkaiyākiya
       intiravikārattaic cārntu aṅkē tavañceytu koṇṭirunta muṉivar
       nūṟṟuvaraic caraṇamaṭaintu tuṟaviyāyiṉaṉ. am mācāttuvāṉ
       maṉaiviyākiya kōvalaṉtāyō, taṉ celvapputalvaṉukku nērnta vipattaip
       poṟātavaḷāy aḷaviṟanta tuyarāl nontu uyirviṭṭaṉaḷ. iṉik kaṇṇakiyiṉ
       tātaiyāṉa mānāykaṉ tavamuṉivarākiya ācīvakaraiyaṭaintu taṉ
       poruḷkaḷāṟ puṇṇiya tāṉaṅkaḷaip purintu tuṟavupūṇṭaṉaṉ. avaṉ
       maṉaiviyākiya kaṇṇakiyiṉ tāy cila nāḷkaḷuḷḷē uyir nīttaṉaḷ.
       icceytiyellāṅ kēṭṭa kōvalaṉ kaṇikaiyākiya mātavi peritun tukkittu,
       tuṉpam viḷaivikkum parattaiyarkōlattilē taṉmakaḷ maṇimēkalaiyaip
       pukaviṭātapaṭi taṉ tāyāṉa cittirāpatikkuk kūṟi viṭṭu, mālaiyuṭaṉ
       taṉ kūntalaiyuṅkaḷaintu pikṣuṇiyākip pauttavikāramaṭaintu
       tarumōpatēcam peṟṟaṉaḷ. iṅṅaṉamāka, yāṉ maturaiyiliruntu koṇarnta

       muṉṉikaḻntavai yellām viḷaṅkac ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉukkuk kūṟimuṭittaṉaṉ.
       Page 71
       ivaṟṟaik kēṭṭirunta avvañci vēntaṉ 'nāṉmaṟaiyāḷa! pāṇṭiyaṉ
       tāṉpurinta koṭuṅkōṉmaiyai niṉaintu uyirnīttapiṉṉar ap pāṇṭināṭṭil
       nikaḻnta vicēṭam eṉṉai?' eṉṟu ucāviṉāṉ. māṭalaṉum aracaṉainōkki,
       'cōḻar kuṭikkuriya tāyattāroṉpatiṉmar tammiloṉṟukūṭi niṉmaittuṉac
       cōḻaṉākiya kiḷḷiyōṭu pakaittu avaṉatu iḷavaraciyalaiyēṟṟu
       ēvalkēḷātu cōṇāṭṭaip peritum alaittuvantakālaiyil,
       avvoṉpatiṉmaruṭaṉum porutu orupakalil avarkaḷai yaḻittu
       maittuṉaṉatu āññācakkarattai oruvaḻiyil niṟuviyavaṉum,
       paḻaiyaṉmāṟaṉatu kāvaṉmaramākiya vēmpai aṭiyōṭu aḻittuveṉṟavaṉum,
       pōntaikkaṇṇi yuṭaiyavaṉumākiya poṟaiyaṉē! kēṭṭaruḷka; koṟkai
       nakarattē iḷavaracāy viḷaṅkiya veṟṟivēṟceḻiyaṉ eṉpavaṉ taṉṉāṭṭukku
       nērnta vipattaiyaṟintu peruñciṉaṅkoṇṭu, orumulaikuṟaitta tirumā
       pattiṉiyākiya kaṇṇakikkup poṟkollar āyiravaraip paliyiṭuvittuppiṉ,

       cantiravamicattōṉākiya av viḷañceḻiyaṉ ēṟi viḷaṅkuvāṉāyiṉaṉ;aracē!

       Page 72
       kaṅkaikkaraip pāṭiyilē ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ ivaṟṟaiyellām neṭunāṭkaḷukkuppiṉ

       virintañālattaip pēriruḷ viḻuṅkumpaṭi vanta mālaikkālattē, centīp
       parantatupōṉṟa mēlaitticai viḷakkameyta veṇpiṟai tōṉṟiyatu.
       aṅṅaṉameḻunta piṟaiyaip peruntakaiyāṉa ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ nōkkiṉāṉ.appōtu
       pakkattirunta nimittikaṉ (peruṅkaṇi) camayamaṟintu aracaṉai vāḻtti,
       'vēntar vēntē! vañciyiṉiṉṟum tēvarīr puṟappaṭṭu iṉṟōṭu
       muppattiraṇṭu mātaṅkaḷākiṉṟaṉa' eṉṟāṉ. eṉalum, ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ
       akkaṅkaippāṭiyil, maramuḷaikaḷāl oḻuṅkāka niraikkappaṭṭup
       paṭaṅkukaḷaiyē matilākavuṭaiya tērvītiyuḷḷē, ciṟitum peritumāyk
       kuṉṟukaḷaik kaṇṭāṟpōl viḷaṅkum kūṭakāraṅkaḷamainta muṭukkiṉ oru
       pakkamākac ceṉṟu, vēlaippāṭu mikkatum cittiravitāṉa mamaintatumāṉa
       attāṇi maṇṭapattai aṭaintu, āṅkuḷḷa poṟciṅkātaṉattē vīṟṟiruntu
       vāyil kāvalarāl māṭalamaṟaiyōṉai āṅkaḻaikkac ceytaṉaṉ. avaṉ
       vantatum, ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ avvantaṇaṉai nōkki 'eṉ maittuṉaṉākiya
       cōḻaṉuṭaṉ pakaitta iḷaṅkōvēntar pōril iṟantapiṉṉar,
       accōṇāṭṭaracaṉatu koṟṟamum ceṅkōlum kēṭiṉṟiyuḷḷaṉavō'eṉṟu ucāva,
       māṭalaṉum, aracaṉai vāḻtti 'vēntē! tēvarum viyappat tūṅkeyil
       mūṉṟaiyum (ivaṉ ākācattiṟ carittuvanta acurarkaḷatu
       mūṉṟeyilkaḷaiyum aḻittavaṉeṉṟu tamiḻnūlkaḷiṟ pukaḻappaṭuvaṉ.)
       eṟintavaṉatu vēlveṟṟiyum, kuṟunaṭaiyuṭaiya puṟāviṉ peruntuyaramum
       ataṉait turattivanta paruntiṉiṭumpaiyum
       Page 73
       oruṅkunīṅkat taṉṉuṭampaiyē arintu tulaiyiṟ pukuntōṉatu
       (cipiccakravartti; muṉṉavaṉum ivaṉum cōḻavamicat talaivarkaḷātalāl,
       ivarkaḷ ceyalkaḷaic ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉuṭaiya maittuṉac cōḻaṉmēl
       ēṟṟiyupacarittāreṉka.) ceṅkōlum, māṟuṅkālamum uṇṭāmō? kāviriyāṟ
       purakkappaṭum cōṇāṭṭuvēntaṟku attuṉpakkālattuṅ kēṭillai' eṉṟu
       kūṟiṉaṉ. ivvāṟu māṭalaṉ collakkēṭṭuc ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ makiḻvuṟṟut
       taṉṉiṟaiyaḷavāka aimpatutulāpāram poṉṉai am maṟaiyavaṉukkut
       tāṉañceytu vekumāṉittaṉaṉ. itumuṭintatum cērar perumāṉ taṉ
       yāttiraikku utavipurinta āriyavaracarākiya kaṉṉar nūṟṟuvaraiyum
       avaruṭaiya vaḷamikunta nāṭṭukkuc cellumāṟu viṭuttup, piṉ taṉ
       tūtuvarāyiravarai aḻaippittut tamiḻaratu pērāṟṟalaiyaṟiyātu
       pōrkkōlaṅkoṇṭuvantu tōṟṟut tāpatavēṭam pūṇṭoḷitta irācakumārarait
       tamiḻaracarāṉa cōḻapāṇṭiyarkkuk kāṭṭivarumāṟu āṇaiyiṭṭu avarkaḷai
       muṉṉatākap pirayāṇappaṭutti aṉuppiviṭṭup piṉ taṉ ciramam nīṅkap
       paḷḷimēvit tuyil koḷvāṉāyiṉaṉ. iṅku ivvāṟāka:-
       cērarājatāṉiyākiya vañcimānakariṟ celvamikunta araṇmaṉaiyuḷ
       vāṉaḷāviya antappurattē, muttukkaḷālākiya calliyum tūkkumivaikaḷāl
       muḻutum vaḷaikkappaṭṭatum vicittiramāṉa mēṟkaṭṭiyamaintatum
       maṇiniraikaḷai iṭaiyiṭaiyē vakuttu vayiramaḻuttappeṟṟa
       poṟṟakaṭṭiṉoḷi oḻukappeṟṟatum, vēlaippāṭumikka puṭaitiraṇṭa
       poṟkālaiyuṭaiyatumāṉa aḻakiya periya amaḷikkaṭṭiliṉ mēl,
       Page 74
       puṇarcciyuṟṟa aṉṉaṅkaḷatu puḷakippāl vīḻnta iḷantōkaikaḷaic ceṟittu
       iraṭṭaiyāka virikkappeṟṟa paḷḷiyiṭaiyē, kōpperuntēviyākiya iḷaṅkō
       vēṇmāḷ tūkkameṉpatiṉṟit taṉittirukka, attuyaraic ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉatu
       vaṭanāṭṭu veṟṟiyai aṟintavarākiya cevilimārkaḷ 'aṉṉāy!
       kātaṟkoḻunaṉaik kāṇātirunta niṉkavalaiyai iṉi yoḻiyakkaṭavāy" eṉṟu
       kūṟip pācuraṅkaḷōṭu cērttup pallāṇṭu pāṭuvārāyiṉar. avvāṟē,
       aracikku ūḻiyañceyyuñ cintaruṅ kūṉaruñ ceṉṟu aṭi vaṇaṅki 'tēvī!
       emperumāṉ vantuviṭṭaṉaṉ; iṉi nī mukamalarcciyuṭaṉ kūntalil
       nāḷoppaṉaipeṟṟu nalampeṟa viḷaṅkuka' eṉṟār. iṅṅaṉam āyattār
       araciyiṉ pirivāṟṟānilaiyai orucār āṟṟiniṟka, malaikaḷiṟ
       puṉaṅkāvalceyyuṅ kāṉavaṉ āṅku mūṅkiliṟkaṭṭappaṭṭa tēṉaiyuṇṭu
       kaḷittu akkaḷippāl kavaṇviṭṭuk kāval puritalai nekiḻntamaiyiṉ,
       accamayamaṟintu ceḻittapuṉak katirkaḷai uṇṇutaṟku vanta
       periyayāṉaikaḷ nalla tuyilaṭaiyumpaṭi malaimakaḷir puṉapparaṇ
       mēliruntu koṇṭu 'vaṭa ticaicceṉṟu vākaiyuntumpaiyuñ cūṭiya
       pōrkkaḷiṟukaḷ tirumpumvaḻi curuṅkak kaṭavatu' eṉṟu tāntām
       aṟintavāṟu pāṭiya kuṟiñcip pāṭṭukkaḷum, 'vaṭavaracaratu
       kōṭṭaikaḷait takarttuk kaḻutaikaḷai ērilē pūṭṭiyuḻutu
       koḷḷaivitaitta uḻavaṉākiya kuṭavarkōmāṉ nāḷaivantu
       viṭuvāṉ; ātalāṟ pakaṭukaḷē! nukampūṇṭuḻutu nāṭṭaip
       paṇpaṭuttuvīrāka; pakaimaṉṉaraic ciṟainīkkum avaṉ piṟantanā
       ḷoppaṉaiyum varukiṉṟatu' eṉṟu pāṭum uḻavaratu oliyamainta
       pāṭalkaḷum,
       Page 75
       aracaṉatu āṉiraikaḷaik kākkum kōvalarkaḷ, kuḷirnta āṉporunaiyāṟṟil
       nīrāṭumakaḷirāl viṭappaṭṭa vaṇṇamuñcuṇṇamum malarum parantu
       intiraviṟpōl viḷaṅkukiṉṟa periya tuṟaiyarukiluḷḷa
       tāḻaimaraṅkaḷiṉmēl iruntukoṇṭu tam pacukkaḷai ap peruntuṟaiyiṟ
       paṭiyaviṭṭut tāmaraikuvaḷai mutaliya pūkkaḷait talaiyiṟcūṭiyavarāy
       'āṉiraikaḷē! villavaṉākiya nam vēntaṉ vantaṉaṉ; avaṉ imayap
       pakkattiṉiṉṟu koṇarnta perutta pacuniraikaḷōṭu nīrum nāḷaiccērntu
       makiḻakkaṭavīr’ eṉṉuṅ karuttuppaṭa ūtum āyaratu vēyṅkuḻalōcaiyum,
       veṇṭiraikaḷāl mōtappaṭṭa kaṭaṟkaraikkaḻikaḷiṉ pakkattuḷḷa
       puṉṉaikkīḻ valampuric caṅkamīṉṟa muttukkaḷē kaḻaṅkāka
       neytaṉilamakaḷir taṅ kaikaḷil ēntikkoṇṭu, neṭunāṭpirinta nam
       maraciyōṭu kūṭimakiḻumpaṭi vāṉavaṉākiya nam vēntaṉ veṟṟiyōṭum
       mīṇṭaṉaṉ; avaṉ cūṭiya tumpaiyaiyum paṉampūvaiyum vañcinakaraiyum,
       naṅkaimīr! nām pāṭuvōmāka’ eṉṟu nuḷaicciyar pāṭiya iṉiya
       pāṭalkaḷumāka nālvakai nilaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟum eḻunta iṉṉicaikaḷaik kūrntu
       kavaṉittukkoṇṭu uṟaṅkātirunta kōpperuntēviyāṉavaḷ, taṉ
       kaivaḷaikaḷaic ceṟit taṇintukoḷḷavum, nakaril valampuric caṅkaṅkaḷ
       valamākaveḻuntu muḻaṅkavum, ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ muttumālaikaḷamainta
       veṇkoṟṟakkuṭaiyiṉ kīḻ vākaiyaṇinta ceṉṉiyōṭum taṉ paṭṭattiyāṉaiyiṉ
       mēl viḷaṅkiyavaṉāy,kuñcaraṅkaḷ pūṭṭiya irataṅkaḷuṭaṉ
       kōnakar muḻutum vantetirkoḷḷa vañcimūtūriṟ piravēcañceytu taṉ
       kōyilai aṭaivāṉāyiṉaṉ.
